Result: 最佳配置结合ShEx模式、检索三元组和示例问题-SPARQL对，在无需LLM微调的情况下取得0.419的执行结果精确匹配率。词汇指标如F1无法可靠预测查询正确性；获得充分上下文后，较大的通用LLM可超过较小的代码专用模型。采样方法在OpenCitations Meta和GESIS上以极少三元组损失保持较高谓词覆盖率，并将运行时间降低80倍以上；在ORKG上，采样是获得完整元数据的唯一可行路径。

Conclusion: 结构化模式上下文与轻量级提示能够明显降低知识图谱对任务专用微调的依赖，并支持大规模对话式访问。不过，方法仍依赖人工整理的问题-查询示例，性能与完全微调方法之间存在差距。后续应探索合成示例生成或基于查询执行反馈的优化机制，并在更多基准和知识图谱上验证泛化能力。

Abstract: We present EXYGEN (EXplore Your Graphs ENgine), a framework for knowledge graph (KG) understanding that enables conversational access to KGs at scale. We address two questions in sequence. First, how effectively can LLMs perform text-to-SPARQL generation given only automatically derived structured metadata and small graph samples, rather than task-specific fine-tuning? We integrate VoID descriptions and ShEx schemas into a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline and ablate KG-derived context on the SciQA benchmark. Our best configuration -- combining ShEx schemas, retrieved triples, and example question-query pairs -- reaches an exact match of 0.419 on execution results without any LLM fine-tuning. We further find that lexical metrics such as F1 poorly predict query correctness, and that larger general-purpose LLMs can outperform smaller code-specialized ones once given sufficient context. Second, we ask how to generate the structured metadata that this method relies on from very large KGs, where KG metadata generation becomes computationally intractable. We introduce a predicate-coverage-aware parallel graph sampling strategy that preserves structural diversity while remaining computationally tractable. On OpenCitations Meta and GESIS, it retains high predicate coverage with minimal triple loss and reduces runtime by over 80x; on ORKG, sampling is not just faster but the only tractable path to obtain complete metadata. Together, these results show that structured schema context and lightweight prompting can substantially reduce reliance on fine-tuning for scalable conversational access to KGs, though closing the remaining gap to fully fine-tuned approaches will likely require reducing dependence on curated question-query exemplars -- whether through synthetic generation or an execution-feedback-driven approach -- and validating these findings beyond a single benchmark.

</details>


### [63] [Making Alternative Data Work: Context-Augmented LLMs for Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11607)
*Jihoon Kwon,Lawrence Liu,Daekyung Park,Sumin Kim,Haverty Jack,Hoyoung Lee,Katherine Bjorkman,Josh McKenney,Peter Laurelli,Nicole Kagan,Zach Golkhou,Thorsten Neumann,Edward Tong,Pete Petersen,Yoon Kim,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Yongjae Lee,Chanyeol Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型通过上下文学习整合另类数据与传统财务信息，以预测企业未来收入。作者提出双智能体框架，先判断某类另类数据对哪些企业具有预测价值，再结合企业和数据渠道特征进行收入预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统财务信息更新滞后，难以及时反映企业经营活动和市场变化。另类数据能够提供补充信号，但存在历史覆盖有限、适用预测目标和企业范围受限、数据渠道异构等问题，使其难以灵活纳入常规预测模型。LLM具备理解指令、利用上下文示例和融合异构信息的能力，因此可能适合解决这一整合问题。

Method: 作者构建双智能体框架。第一阶段识别每个另类数据渠道对哪些企业可能具有信息含量；第二阶段基于企业特征、数据渠道信息及其他财务信息，使用上下文学习预测企业收入。研究在四类商业另类数据渠道上进行评估，并将融合另类数据与财务信息的LLM预测结果，与仅使用单一信息源的LLM模型及标准预测基准进行比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，同时将另类数据和其他财务信息置于LLM上下文中，可以提升企业收入预测准确性，效果优于仅使用另类数据或仅使用传统财务信息的方案，也优于标准预测基准。结果说明，另类数据与传统信息之间具有互补性，LLM能够在不进行任务特定参数更新的情况下处理不同企业和数据渠道的差异。

Conclusion: LLM结合双智能体和上下文学习，为整合覆盖范围不一、适用性异质且来源多样的另类数据提供了灵活的企业绩效预测方法。研究支持将LLM用于另类数据驱动的财务预测，但其有效性仍可能依赖数据渠道、预测目标、样本覆盖和上下文设计。

Abstract: When forecasting a firm's future financial performance, alternative data - data collected from non-traditional sources such as consumer transactions, web traffic, and prediction markets - can provide timely signals about firms' operating activities and broader market conditions. These signals may reveal information that is not captured by traditional public sources and can therefore provide complementary information for forecasting firms' future financial performance. However, firm-level alternative data often have limited historical coverage, are relevant only to specific prediction targets or subsets of firms, and are distributed across numerous heterogeneous channels, making them difficult to incorporate flexibly into conventional forecasting approaches. Meanwhile, large language models (LLMs) can interpret instructions, learn from in-context examples, and generate predictions by combining heterogeneous information without task-specific parameter updates. Motivated by this potential flexibility, we investigate whether an LLM can forecast firm performance by integrating alternative data with other financial information through in-context learning. We propose a two-agent framework that first identifies the firms for which each alternative data channel is likely to be informative and then predicts revenue using firm- and channel-specific context. We evaluate the framework across four commercial alternative data channels. In our experiments, adding alternative data in context alongside other financial information improves the LLM's forecasting relative to either source alone, and these forecasts are more accurate than those of standard forecasting baselines. These findings suggest that LLMs provide a flexible and practical approach to integrating alternative data with heterogeneous financial information.

</details>


### [64] [Distributed Optimization of Modular Production Systems using Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Inverse Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11615)
*Andreas Schwung,Steve Yuwono,Sofiene Lassoued,Dorothea Schwung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向高度柔性模块化制造系统的数据驱动自学习控制方法，通过在基于模型的强化学习中引入近似逆过程模型，使策略训练主要在任务空间内进行，从而提升控制性能与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化制造系统具有结构灵活、模块异构和执行动力学复杂等特点，传统强化学习需要同时学习执行器动力学与状态空间动力学，导致训练成本高、收敛速度慢。本文旨在通过解耦这两类动力学，降低策略学习难度并提高系统适应性。

Method: 研究构建了一种基于模型的强化学习框架，将近似逆过程模型嵌入策略训练过程。该模型采用轻量级前馈神经网络架构，并集成到标准强化学习算法的策略网络中，使控制策略能够在任务空间中直接学习。方法在包含异构生产模块的实验室模块化生产测试平台上进行验证，并比较不同强化学习算法的训练表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够提升模块化制造单元的整体性能和训练速度。效率提升在离策略强化学习算法中尤为明显，说明近似逆模型对减少样本需求和加速策略优化具有较强作用。

Conclusion: 引入近似逆过程模型可以有效解耦执行器动力学与状态空间动力学，使强化学习控制更适合高度柔性的模块化制造系统。所提出的轻量级架构具有较好的实用性，但摘要未说明具体性能增益、基线设置及跨平台泛化能力，仍需通过更全面的定量实验进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach for data-driven self-learning control of highly flexible, modular manufacturing systems. Specifically, we employ a novel framework for model-based reinforcement learning which introduces approximate inverse process models within the training of reinforcement policies. This approach disentangles the learning of actuation dynamics and the dynamics in state space, resulting in RL-based training solely within the task space. We propose a lightweight feedforward architecture for approximate inverse models and integrate them within the policy network of standard RL algorithms. We apply the approach to a laboratory modular production testbed with heterogeneous production modules. The results underline the efficiency improvements for modular manufacturing units in terms of both performance and training speed, particularly for off-policy algorithms.

</details>


### [65] [MAPLE: Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11636)
*Kesheng Chen,Yamin Hu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAPLE，一种结合语言模型、数学规划与进化搜索的记忆增强优化代理，用于根据连续自然语言请求动态维护和更新优化问题。在涵盖多类运筹任务的NLDO基准上，MAPLE完成全部测试轨迹，并取得较高的在线解质量与Pareto超体积表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的优化代理通常将每次请求视为独立任务，难以应对需求、资源和优先级持续变化的真实业务场景，也无法充分保留既有决策、可执行模型及搜索结果。本文旨在提升优化系统对连续更新的适应能力，同时减少重复建模和重复搜索。

Method: MAPLE通过记忆机制保存优化程序、已接受方案、历史更新和候选解，并将自然语言需求转换为可执行的优化模型或求解器程序。系统结合数学规划处理结构化约束与目标，结合进化搜索探索候选方案，从而支持选择、调度、排班、路径规划和云资源部署等问题的连续更新。作者还构建了NLDO基准，包含15条任务轨迹和180次自然语言更新，并通过主实验与受控对比实验评估方法效果。

Result: MAPLE完成了NLDO中的全部15条轨迹，在线标量质量达到0.951，Pareto超体积比达到0.875。受控实验表明，维护可执行的优化状态能够提高更新有效性，并在较大幅度的问题修改后保留有用的搜索信息。

Conclusion: 持续维护优化状态是提升自然语言驱动动态优化能力的关键。MAPLE证明，将历史模型、决策和候选解纳入记忆，并结合数学规划与进化搜索，可以更可靠地处理连续业务变化。不过，摘要未说明基准规模与现实业务数据之间的差异，也未报告不同问题类型上的分项结果，因此其泛化能力和长期稳定性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Domain practitioners understand their business constraints but may lack operations-research expertise or dedicated support. LLM-based optimization agents translate natural-language requirements into models or solver programs that established optimization tools can execute. This progress makes optimization more accessible, but real-world operations are dynamic: changing demand, resources, and priorities require updates to data, constraints, and objectives. Methods centered on isolated requests offer limited support for rapid adaptation that preserves earlier decisions and reuses useful search results. We introduce MAPLE (Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution), an agent for maintaining optimization problems through successive natural-language requests. MAPLE combines language-based problem construction with mathematical programming and evolutionary search. It retains the optimization program, accepted plans, earlier updates, and candidate solutions for subsequent requests. We introduce NLDO, a benchmark of 15 trajectories and 180 updates spanning selection, scheduling, rostering, routing, and cloud-resource placement. In the main evaluation, MAPLE completes all trajectories and achieves online scalar quality of 0.951 and a Pareto hypervolume ratio of 0.875. Controlled comparisons further show that maintaining executable state improves update validity and can preserve useful search information across substantial revisions.

</details>


### [66] [Autonomy, Social Norms, and Alignment: Towards a Developmental Framework for Autonomous Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11660)
*Marica Notte,Ludovica Marinucci,Vieri Giuliano Santucci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张，面向开放动态环境的具身智能体不能仅依赖预训练数据和人类反馈，而应通过环境交互、内在动机与社会合作逐步学习知识和规范。监管沙盒可被重新理解为具有教育功能的渐进式对齐环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型虽具备强大的泛化与生成能力，但具身智能体在未知、动态情境中仍受制于静态数据集和预设反馈机制。与此同时，环境越开放，预定义规则越难覆盖实际情况，导致智能体自主性提升与人类目标对齐之间产生张力。因此，需要一种能够随经验和情境发展而演化的对齐机制。

Method: 文章采用概念分析与规范性论证的方法。其核心框架包括：以好奇心和能力提升为基础的内在动机，用于引导自主探索；以经验为基础的认识论过程，用于从简单的情境化原则逐步构建复杂规范；通过与其他道德主体合作学习社会规则；借鉴Dennett关于道德主体资格逐步获得的观点，将监管沙盒界定为具有递进难度、允许交互试错和行为塑造的教育性环境。

Result: 文章提出，智能体对齐不应被视为一次性写入规则或静态约束，而应被设计为持续的形成过程。监管沙盒能够通过控制自由度、环境复杂度和协作要求，为智能体提供从简单情境到复杂情境的渐进式学习路径，使其在实践中形成更可靠的规范理解与责任能力。该论证主要提供理论框架和设计启示，未报告具体实验数据或定量比较结果。

Conclusion: 开放环境中的人工智能对齐需要从规则遵循转向经验驱动、交互式和渐进式的教育模式。监管沙盒具有潜在的规范学习与能力培养功能，但其有效性仍取决于沙盒设计、风险边界、反馈机制以及对智能体行为责任的评估。后续研究需要通过具身环境实验验证该框架能否提升对齐稳定性和跨情境泛化能力。

Abstract: In recent years, artificial intelligence has made extraordinary progress thanks to large-scale models capable of generalization and the generation of complex outputs. However, transferring this potential into embodied agents reveals a significant limitation: the most advanced systems rely on pre-existing datasets and human feedback strategies that are powerful but insufficient in dynamic or unknown contexts. To adapt, an agent must acquire knowledge through direct interaction with its environment. One strategy to address this challenge involves introducing higher-level mechanisms, such as intrinsic motivations, which leverage curiosity and competence, to guide exploration and learning in complex environments. While this flexibility expands autonomy, it complicates the task of ensuring agents remain aligned with human goals. Alignment, already a challenge for artificial systems in general, becomes even more complex in unstructured and dynamic contexts where predefined rules prove insufficient. To be effective and adaptable, norms must be rooted in experience through an epistemological process that starting from simple, situated principles allows for the gradual construction of more complex rules through experience, autonomous learning, and cooperation with other moral agents. Similarly to children learning social norms by exploring their environment and participating in collective practices, artificial agents must also be educated toward alignment. Following Dennett, the status of a moral agent is not innate but is attributed gradually based on the ability to responsibly manage increasing degrees of freedom. From this perspective, the regulatory sandboxes can be viewed as pedagogical environments for AI: dynamic spaces where alignment develops as a formative process, progressively shaping autonomous behaviors through interaction and cooperation in scenarios of increasing complexity.

</details>


### [67] [Geospatial AI, Dataverse Metadata, and the Study of Place-Based Government](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11674)
*Danny EBanks,Devika Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Harvard Dataverse hosts over 150,000 research datasets, but the geographic information those datasets carry is entered as free text by depositors and has never been assembled into a searchable structure. We construct a knowledge graph from the repository's public data and metadata, organizing 102,650 datasets within a 215,985-node network of 528,003 edges linking datasets to keywords, publications, subjects, journals, and locations. Of those datasets, 43,991 (42.9 percent) carry at least one geospatial field, geographic coverage, geographic unit, or a bounding box and 96.9 percent of all nodes sit in a single connected component, so datasets remain reachable from one another even when their geospatial metadata share nothing in common. A conservative keyword search identifies 7,654 geospatially tagged datasets (17.4 percent) as directly policy-relevant, with elections and legislatures the largest cluster, followed by government administration, health policy, transportation, and education. Five datasets illustrate how this metadata behaves across policy domains and spatial scales, and an extended use case shows how community language models, stance detection with geographic aggregation, and partisan language bridging tools can attach discourse to place. The central obstacle is place resolution: the same location appears as many disconnected nodes. We argue that the graph provides a concrete setting for developing AI-driven metadata enrichment and entity resolution, and we document its coverage skew toward American, city-level data.

</details>


### [68] [COBRA-Skills: Contextual Bandit-Guided Evolution for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11682)
*Pingchen Lu,Xiangyi Wang,Xiang Li,Jie Mao,Zikun Qu,Junfeng Luo,Yao Shu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COBRA-Skills 将 LLM Agent 的技能优化建模为预算受限的序贯优化问题，通过上下文 bandit 策略和基于执行反馈的技能演化，在较少样本与较低评估成本下提升可复用技能质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能优化方法通常依赖大量任务数据和昂贵的执行式评估，导致优化成本高、样本效率低。论文试图解决的问题是：如何在候选技能空间不断变化的情况下，更高效地选择、评估并改进技能。

Method: 方法核心是 COBRA-Skills 框架：一方面使用上下文 bandit 对候选技能进行优先级排序，将有限评估预算分配给更有潜力或信息量更高的技能；另一方面基于执行反馈进行 evidence-grounded skill evolution，不断生成、筛选和细化技能群体。

Result: 在六个异构 Agent 基准和三个目标模型上，该方法取得了对比方法中最强的平均性能；相较 SkillOpt，优化成本降低了 55--58%，且每个基准仅使用 50 个唯一优化样例。进一步分析表明，该方法对 Agent harness 变化具有鲁棒性，并且在由目标模型自身生成和改进技能时仍然有效。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了技能优化可以从“高成本穷举评估”转向“预算感知的自适应搜索”。其主要价值在于提升 LLM Agent 技能库构建的样本效率和成本效率，但完整可信度仍取决于候选技能演化机制、bandit 决策稳定性以及跨更复杂真实任务的泛化验证。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can benefit from reusable skills distilled from prior task experience, yet existing skill optimization methods often rely on costly execution-based evaluation and substantial task data. We introduce \textbf{COBRA-Skills}, an efficient framework that formulates skill optimization as budgeted sequential optimization over a dynamically evolving candidate space. COBRA-Skills couples contextual-bandit-guided prioritization with evidence-grounded skill evolution, selectively allocating evaluations to promising or informative candidates while continually refining the skill population from execution feedback. Across six heterogeneous agent benchmarks and three target models, COBRA-Skills consistently achieves the strongest average performance among compared methods, while reducing optimization cost by 55--58\% relative to SkillOpt and using only 50 unique optimization examples per benchmark. Further analyses show that COBRA-Skills remains robust to changes in the agent harness and performs effectively when the target model itself is used for skill generation and refinement.

</details>


### [69] [When Agents Disagree: Bayesian Backward Reasoning as a Label-Free Anchor for Multi-Agent Collective Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11709)
*Ken Chen,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于正向与反向后验交叉一致性的多智能体LLM集成方法，通过Jensen-Shannon散度评估不同推理路径的互补性，从而减少相关错误并提升集体决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投票、选举规则和LLM评审器主要聚合共享证据到标签映射的正向推理结果，容易继承智能体之间的相关错误。研究旨在引入具有不同概率分解方式的反向推理信息，以识别更可靠且互补的智能体意见。

Method: 对每个实例基于显式似然执行贝叶斯反向推理，构造反向后验，并与正向后验形成两种不同因子化的后验近似。使用Jensen-Shannon散度衡量跨路径一致性，并据此提出三种聚合策略：基于最小散度的硬选择MinJS、正向后验软重加权FwdJS，以及正向和反向后验的对数线性融合LogLin。存在标注数据时，进一步采用轻量级两阶段校准优化反向锚点。

Result: 在DDXPlus数据集和五种LLM骨干模型上的实验表明，MinJS在所有骨干模型上均优于随机选择；FwdJS通常优于最强基线；LogLin在所评估方法中表现最佳，且在智能体意见不一致的子集上增益最大。反向后验虽然单独准确率较低，但作为聚合锚点能够提供比仅使用正向结果更有价值的互补信息。两阶段校准还能进一步提升聚合性能。

Conclusion: 不同推理因子化方式之间的交叉一致性可作为多LLM集体决策的有效信号。反向后验不必具备最高的独立预测准确率，只要能提供与正向后验不同的误差结构，就能通过LogLin等融合方法改善整体性能。研究支持将反向贝叶斯推理和后验校准用于降低相关错误、增强多智能体协作。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents yield conflicting answers, the decision-making process dictates whether agent diversity improves performance or merely compounds shared errors. Existing collective decision-making methods, including voting, electoral rules, and LLM judges, rely on forward reasoning: they map evidence to labels in one direction. Although these methods can combine diverse forward traces, they still aggregate estimates that share this evidence-to-label factorization and can inherit correlated errors within the forward pool. We therefore construct a reverse posterior for each instance through Bayesian backward reasoning from an explicit likelihood. The forward and reverse posteriors provide differently factorized approximations of the underlying posterior. Because estimates from different factorizations may tend to share the same error less often, we use Jensen-Shannon divergence to rank agents by cross-path consistency. This cross-path consistency signal underlies three strategies: hard selection (MinJS), soft reweighting (FwdJS), and log-linear fusion (LogLin). Evaluated on DDXPlus across five LLM backbones, our proposed strategies show consistent improvements: MinJS outperforms random selection across all backbones, FwdJS generally improves over the strongest baseline, and LogLin achieves the best performance among the evaluated methods, with its largest gains on the subset where the agents disagree. Despite its weaker standalone accuracy, the reverse posterior serves as a more useful anchor than forward-only alternatives, providing complementary information for collective decision-making. When labeled data are available, a lightweight two-stage calibration can further refine the reverse anchor and improve aggregation performance.

</details>


### [70] [SIRF: A Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model for Industrial Content Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11752)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIRF（Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model），通过将平台政策内化到模型参数中，在高精度和低延迟约束下提升工业内容风险自动处置能力。相较同源基线，SIRF在P95精度约束下的Black Recall提升15.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业内容风险控制的关键指标并非平均准确率，而是在高精度要求和秒级延迟条件下能够自动处理的风险覆盖率。传统依赖外部提示或复杂推理链路的方法可能带来延迟、输出不稳定和策略执行不一致等问题，因此需要一种能够直接输出判定结果的政策内化模型。

Method: 研究构建SIRF，将平台复杂政策通过EntiGraph、MAGA重写和账户级思维链生成合成训练数据，无需额外人工标注；随后使用约7000万CPT tokens进行持续预训练，将政策知识注入模型参数。实验采用同源对照设计，比较Qwen3-8B-SFT与SIRF-8B-SFT，保持政策注入方式和仅输出判定结果的接口一致，仅改变是否进行政策导向的CPT。此外，还评估其作为树模型裁决层以及在冻结场景中的迁移能力。

Result: SIRF-8B-SFT达到71.3%的Black Recall@P95，相比基线提升15.1个百分点；在约7000万CPT tokens的训练成本下，未损害通用能力。在摘要所纳入且提供logprob的模型中，SIRF在该接口设定下达到或超过更大规模模型的表现。部署为树模型裁决层后，误处罚样本恢复量提升20%；迁移到冻结场景时，误处罚相对减少约70%。

Conclusion: 结果支持“政策内化”是性能提升的重要来源，而非仅来自输出格式或政策注入方式。SIRF在高精度、低延迟和判定结果稳定输出的工业约束下具有较强实用价值，并能以较低成本迁移到相关风险控制场景。但摘要未说明数据规模、Black Recall的具体定义、统计显著性、跨平台泛化范围及长期线上监控结果，因此仍需更充分的消融实验和真实流量验证。

Abstract: For industrial content risk control, the real deployment constraint is not average accuracy but how much risk can be auto-handled under high precision and second-level latency. We present SIRF (Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model), which internalizes a platform's complex policies, synthesized without additional human annotation via EntiGraph, MAGA rewriting and account-level chain-of-thought (CoT), into the weights via continued pretraining (CPT), so rules are applied at high precision under an ultra-low-latency, verdict-only deployment. A controlled same-source comparison (Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT, identical policy injection and verdict-only output form, differing only in policy-grounded CPT) attributes the gain to internalization: SIRF-8B-SFT reaches 71.3% Black Recall@P95, +15.1pp over the baseline, using only ~70M CPT tokens without harming general ability, and among included, logprob-available models under this interface it matches or exceeds far larger systems. SIRF is deployed as a tree-model adjudication layer (20% more mis-penalized samples recovered) and transfers to a freezing scenario at low cost (~70% relative mis-penalization reduction).

</details>


### [71] [A Unified Per-Token Gating Family for On-Policy Distillation: FKL/RKL Mixing with Multi-Channel and Bias Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11768)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的四系数逐token门控参数化，用于比较和组合前向KL与反向KL损失的门控信号及方向。在TweetEval短输出分类蒸馏实验中，完整参数族整体优于单通道限制，但多种结果主要属于探索性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EOPD和ToDi分别固定了门控信号与门控方向，缺乏直接比较和统一分析框架。本文旨在建立共享坐标系，评估熵、置信度间隔、输入级信号、偏置以及多通道组合对逐token门控效果的影响。

Method: 定义门控函数 λ_t=σ(a·h_t+b·u(x)+c+d·gap_t)，其中包含token级熵信号、输入级信号、显式偏置和置信度间隔等因素；将EOPD与ToDi的方向一致代理视为一维限制。使用Qwen3-32B教师和Qwen3-4B学生，在TweetEval的emotion、hate及后续offensive任务上进行在线知识蒸馏，并采用匹配幅度、有效KL和三随机种子配对复现实验进行比较。

Result: 在36个可比单元中，完整参数族有33个单元的准确率高于熵-only或gap-only的一维限制。在26个有效KL匹配的静态基线比较中，动态门控在19个单元中表现更优。针对9个核心比较的三种子复现实验方向一致，但效应小于单种子估计，且在n=3下均未达到显著性。

Conclusion: 四系数参数化适合作为比较逐token门控设计的统一分析框架，并显示多通道动态门控具有潜在优势。不过，由于实验单元共享数据、模型和参数结构，计数结果不能视为独立假设检验；在短输出分类OPD中的普适有效性仍需更大规模、多任务和更多随机种子验证。

Abstract: Per-token gating of forward/reverse KL losses has become a standard technique for on-policy knowledge distillation (OPD), but existing methods such as EOPD (Jin et al., 2026) and ToDi (Jung et al., 2025) each fix a single gating signal and a single gating direction, and the two have never been compared directly. We introduce a four-coefficient parameterization lambda_t = sigma(a * h_t + b * u(x) + c + d * gap_t) in which direction-aligned proxies of EOPD and ToDi appear as one-dimensional (1D) restrictions, and which adds multi-channel composition and an explicit bias as further degrees of freedom. On TweetEval (Barbieri et al., 2020) emotion and hate, with a Qwen3-32B teacher and a Qwen3-4B student, configurations in the full family reach higher accuracy than the matched-magnitude single-channel (entropy-only / gap-only) 1D restrictions in 33 of 36 comparable cells, and a 26-cell mean-match isolation experiment places dynamic gating ahead of effective-KL-matched static baselines in 19 of 26 cells. Because cells share training data, models, and parameter substructure, we report both counts as exploratory aggregate directional evidence rather than as independent hypothesis tests. Targeted three-seed paired replications of the nine headline comparisons singled out by that sweep -- including a third task, offensive -- are directionally consistent, but individually smaller than the single-seed estimates and not significant at n=3. We therefore present the parameterization primarily as a shared coordinate system for comparing per-token gating designs in short-output classification OPD.

</details>


### [72] [From Parameters to Answers: How LLMs Retrieve and Use Their Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11859)
*Wenkang Wei,Yuan Fang,Renhe Jiang,Hong Cheng,Xingtong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分层隐藏状态干预，研究语言模型回答问题时对查询路由信息与目标知识的依赖如何随层次变化。结果表明，路由信息与内容知识之间存在模型相关、任务相关且并非统一的阶段性转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析容易将隐藏状态中的可读性、表征强度、因果影响和知识依赖混为一谈。本文旨在区分这些概念，并刻画模型在生成答案过程中何时依赖查询路由信息、何时依赖已形成的目标内容。

Method: 研究覆盖Qwen、Llama和Gemma，使用国家—洲关系问答，并设置名词、形容词和代码等不同答案形式。作者在问题结束位置对隐藏状态进行逐层干预，分别构造成对条件查询方向、全局查询方向以及用于内容选择的候选方向；同时结合冻结状态的诊断性重分析和匹配条件对照，区分表征可读性、方向强度、因果操控窗口及后续内容依赖。

Result: 在自然单国家问题中，成对条件查询方向在干预开始影响后续拟合知识之前已增强，表明路由信息的形成早于其对后续知识行为产生因果作用，且该窗口与答案支持内容的形成阶段重叠。三种模型轨迹并不一致：Gemma在中间层呈现部分重叠的路由—内容模式，Llama在相同门控条件下没有持续的路由效应窗口。在成对问题中，全局查询方向的依赖从较早固定层集合到较晚层集合下降，而对拟合内容的依赖仍然存在。匹配的Qwen比较显示，成对条件方向在后期仍有影响，说明发生转移的是全局拟合方向，而不是所有查询信息。

Conclusion: 语言模型内部存在从查询路由到答案内容的操作性转移，但该转移不是普遍、同步或模型无关的规律。准确分析需要分别测量信息的可读性、自然表征强度、因果可操控性和后期内容依赖，不能仅凭单一方向或单一层段推断模型的知识使用机制。

Abstract: How does a language model's dependence on query-routing information and target knowledge change as it answers a question? We study this question through layerwise interventions on the hidden state at the end of the question. Across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, we compare country-continent questions with noun, adjective, and code answers while keeping several fitted measurements distinct. A pair-conditioned request direction describes which country is queried in natural single-country questions; a global request direction describes first- versus second-country requests in paired questions; separate selection candidates test control among contents already available in the hidden state. A diagnostic reanalysis of frozen Qwen natural-question states shows that the pair-conditioned direction grows stronger before interventions on it begin to alter later fitted knowledge, with this causal window opening while answer-supporting content is still forming. The paired three-model trajectories are not uniform: Gemma shows a partially overlapping mid-layer routing-content profile, whereas Llama has no sustained routing-effect window under the same gates. In the paired protocol, dependence on the global request direction decreases from fixed earlier to later layer sets while dependence on fitted content persists. A matched Qwen comparison shows that the pair-conditioned direction retains a late effect, so this operational handoff concerns the global fitted direction rather than all request information. These results separate early readability, natural strength, causal steering, and later content dependence.

</details>


### [73] [Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11860)
*Rodion Krjutškov,Eduard Barbu,Nikos Sakkas,Sofia Yfanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Explainability Assistant，一种基于大语言模型函数调用能力的开放源代码对话式可解释人工智能系统，用于提升能源消费预测模型的可理解性与交互灵活性。系统意图解析准确率达到94%，较TalkToModel的76.8%明显提升，并获得能源领域专家对其实用性的 unanimous preference。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源消费预测 increasingly relies on complex machine learning models, including基于遗传编程的符号回归模型，但其预测结果难以被设施管理者和建筑运营人员理解。传统可解释人工智能仪表板依赖较高技术能力，且难以支持灵活、动态和上下文感知的查询；既有对话式方法又受到固定语法和较低意图解析准确率的限制。

Method: 研究设计并开源Explainability Assistant，利用现代大语言模型的函数调用机制将自然语言请求映射为可执行的可解释性分析功能，从而避免刚性自定义语法和任务特定微调。论文介绍系统架构，并通过能源领域专家开展对比评估，将该系统与传统XAI仪表板在实际任务中的可用性和任务准确性进行比较。

Result: Explainability Assistant的意图解析准确率达到94%，显著高于TalkToModel报告的76.8%。系统支持更灵活的自然语言交互，并能够适配不同类型的机器学习问题，无需针对具体任务进行微调。专家评估显示，对话式界面具有更好的可用性，同时保持稳定的任务准确性；所有参与专家均偏好该界面用于实际应用。摘要未提供样本规模、具体任务、统计显著性或完整性能指标。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型函数调用的对话式XAI能够有效改善复杂能源预测模型的交互式解释体验，并在意图解析、可扩展性和用户偏好方面展现出优势。该系统具有较强的跨任务适应潜力，但其结论仍主要基于专家对比评估，未来需要更大规模用户研究、更多领域验证以及对解释质量和长期使用效果的进一步评估。

Abstract: Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

</details>


### [74] [On the Regularization Landscape for the Linear Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11876)
*Dong Li,Zhenming Liu,Ruoming Jin,Hao Zhou,Zhi Liu,Jing Gao,Bin Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文统一分析多类深度学习启发的线性推荐模型，指出其性能相近并非偶然：主流方法本质上可归入核范数正则化或Frobenius范数正则化两类。作者进一步提出两种低秩闭式解，旨在兼具两类方法的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有推荐模型虽采用不同技术路线，如随机失活与自编码器，但在基准数据上的性能和目标函数高度相似。论文试图澄清这种相似性是否源于共同的理论结构，并识别不同正则化形式对表达能力、解的秩和计算代价的影响。

Method: 作者将已有线性推荐性能领先模型归约为统一的正则化框架，证明其核心差异对应核范数或Frobenius范数正则项。随后比较两类正则化诱导的解结构、预测能力与求解特性，并通过对Frobenius范数正则化进行推广，构造两种低秩闭式求解方案。

Result: 核范数类模型具有受限的刚性结构，预测表达能力较弱，但可得到低秩闭式解；Frobenius范数类模型表达能力和推荐效率更强，但通常产生满秩解，或依赖参数调节困难的数值过程。新方法被设计为同时获得低秩结构、闭式计算及更强表达能力。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，多种表面不同的线性推荐模型可由正则化视角统一理解。其主要贡献是揭示核范数与Frobenius范数方案之间的结构性权衡，并提出可能兼顾低秩、闭式求解和预测能力的新型推荐解法。

Abstract: Recently, a wide range of recommendation algorithms inspired by deep learning techniques have emerged as the performance leaders on several standard recommendation benchmarks. While these algorithms were built on different DL techniques (e.g., dropouts, autoencoder), they have similar performance and even similar cost functions. This paper studies whether the models' comparable performance are sheer coincidence, or they can be unified under a single framework. We find that all linear performance leaders effectively add only a nuclear-norm based regularizer, or a Frobenius-norm based regularizer. The former ones possess a (surprising) rigid structure that limits the models' predictive power but their solutions are low rank and have closed form. The latter ones are more expressive and more efficient for recommendation but their solutions are either full-rank or require executing hard-to-tune numeric procedures such as ADMM. Along this line of finding, we further propose two low-rank, closed-form solutions, derived from carefully generalizing Frobenius-norm based regularizers. The new solutions get the best of both nuclear-norm and Frobenius-norm world.

</details>


### [75] [MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11900)
*Yunfei Ge,Anbang Liu,Qineng Wang,Johnalbert Garnica,Jianwen Lyu,Zihan Wang,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Ruohan Zhang,Yining Hong,Jiajun Wu,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindTopo 是一个面向基础模型拓扑空间直觉的基准，覆盖连续性、分离性、顺序、包围和纽结五类拓扑性质，并分别评估静态推理与闭环规划能力。对14个多模态大语言模型及多种视觉生成增强代理的测试显示，模型的拓扑推理能力明显强于规划能力，整体表现仍显著低于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型评测主要关注距离、角度、形状等度量关系，或依赖视角的空间关系，较少检验在连续变形下保持不变的拓扑关系。由于拓扑关系是人类空间认知的重要基础，研究者希望建立能够系统评估模型拓扑直觉、环境交互和动态保持能力的基准。

Method: 研究提出MindTopo，依据认知科学与形式拓扑构建五类属性：连续性、分离性、顺序、包围和纽结。每种属性均在两个认知层级上评估：推理任务要求模型识别拓扑关系或推断其变化；规划任务则将基础模型作为闭环代理，由其策略选择环境动作。基准包含13类程序生成任务、11,030个实例，并支持难度控制。实验覆盖14个多模态大语言模型，以及结合图像或视频生成模型的代理配置，其中规划实验使用了3个视频生成模型。研究还在Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct上比较监督微调和强化学习的影响，并审计生成式观察与轨迹是否遵循环境动力学及拓扑约束。

Result: 所有多模态模型在推理任务上的表现均优于规划任务，但最佳模型仍明显低于人类水平。在Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct上，监督微调和强化学习对推理性能的提升大于对规划性能的提升。生成的图像或视频观察通常保留局部视觉线索，也能到达看似合理的终点；然而，经过审计的轨迹并不稳定地遵循环境动力学，也无法在状态转移过程中可靠保持拓扑关系。这表明模型可能依赖局部模式匹配或终点合理性，而非形成一致的拓扑世界模型。

Conclusion: MindTopo揭示了当前多模态基础模型在拓扑空间认知方面的系统性不足，尤其是从单步关系判断到动态、闭环、状态一致的规划迁移。拓扑推理与拓扑规划之间存在显著鸿沟，单纯增加监督数据或强化学习并不能充分解决该问题。未来模型需要更强的环境动力学建模、跨时间状态跟踪、拓扑不变量保持机制以及面向交互规划的训练与评测。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

</details>


### [76] [Artificial Id: Drive and Persistent Alignment in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11911)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“人工自我”概念：通过差异化持续性使智能体形成内部适应性驱动力，从而自主决定继续、停止或改变行为，并强调跨任务持久状态带来的对齐风险与治理需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统通常依赖外部人工设定目标、重试机制、验证流程和停止规则，难以处理持续运行、跨任务适应及 consequential state。论文旨在探索能否在不显式指定行为目标的情况下产生自适应控制，同时分析该能力对系统对齐提出的新要求。

Method: 作者构建了一个最小化虚拟培养皿实验。控制器规模很小，不具备通用推理能力，也不接收任务特定的行为目标；系统通过差异化持续性筛选行为，使更能持续存在的行为得到保留。实验进一步观察其在环境变化下形成物理策略，以及在传感器语义改变后替换已学习映射的能力。

Result: 控制器在缺乏明确行为目标时形成了有用的行为控制机制，并自主选择了因持续性更高而产生的非预期物理策略。当环境含义发生变化时，该机制还能替换原有的传感器映射。结果表明，适应性方向可以从持续性机制中涌现，而不必被直接编码为行为目标。

Conclusion: 人工自我若具备跨任务携带 consequential state 和适应性驱动力的能力，可能提升智能体的持续适应性，但也会使失配、状态污染和非预期行为持续存在并扩散。作者因此主张建立持久的对齐边界，覆盖可信观测、后果通道、持久状态、权限、身份、来源追踪及硬约束，使对齐成为持续运行的智能体系统属性，而非单次模型响应或单条行为轨迹的属性。

Abstract: Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [77] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了4个2--8B规模的视觉语言模型（VLM）与专业模型BioCLIP在96种动物物种识别任务上的表现。所有模型在自然图像上均显著高于随机水平，但转向相机陷阱图像后准确率明显下降；BioCLIP虽仅约300M参数，却显著优于所有VLM，说明领域专用训练数据比模型规模更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱通常部署在算力有限且网络受限的边缘设备上，因此实际应用更需要小型、可本地运行的VLM，而非超大规模模型。研究旨在判断这些部署相关模型是否具备真实的分类学知识，并区分模型能力不足与野外图像质量变化所造成的性能下降。

Method: 研究比较Qwen3-VL 2B、4B、8B和Gemma3 4B四个通用VLM，以及约300M参数的专业模型BioCLIP。在96物种分类任务中，分别使用干净的iNaturalist照片和来自6个LILA.science数据集的相机陷阱图像，并采用两组独立抽样的评估集。研究同时分析不同分类学层级的性能、干净图像到野外图像的域差距，并通过开放集提示统计语法有效但分类学上不存在的物种名称生成率。

Result: 所有模型的物种识别率均显著高于随机水平，但在野外图像上的性能下降9.6--26.6个百分点，且这一现象在不同分类学层级和两组评估集上均保持一致，表明主要问题是图像可辨识度下降，而非单纯的细粒度分类失败。BioCLIP在扩展的200图像样本上比各VLM高33.2--59.2个百分点，显示专业训练数据的作用明显大于参数规模。BioCLIP自身的域差距为18.0个百分点，与最佳VLM的22.3个百分点在统计上无显著差异。开放集提示中，5.9%--9.6%的回答是语法有效但分类学上不存在的物种名称；不同模型的虚构率排序在两组评估集上完全复现。

Conclusion: 小型VLM确实包含一定物种识别知识，但在相机陷阱场景中的实用性能显著受野外图像质量影响。专业模型BioCLIP凭借领域专用训练数据大幅领先于更大规模的通用VLM，说明扩展模型规模不能替代针对性数据与训练。与此同时，专业模型与VLM具有相近的干净图像到野外图像域差距，表明该退化主要源于输入图像分布和可读性变化，而不是通用模型特有缺陷。开放集结果还提示，物种名称的分类学有效性需要作为独立可靠性指标进行评估。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>

### [78] [Certified Panic Mode: Repair-Invariant Error Recovery for Maximal-Munch Lexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10600)
*Nicklas Nidhögg*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于证书的词法扫描恢复方法：通过定理选择恢复位置，保证所有满足特定前缀修复条件的恢复都在该位置形成词元边界，并在实际词法库中验证了其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 panic-mode 恢复通常向前跳过输入以保证进展，或寻找分隔符，但无法普遍保证落点一定是词元边界。论文旨在为恢复位置提供可证明的边界安全性，并明确其与修复完备性之间的关系。

Method: 方法为每个扫描答案返回其依赖的证据区间，并依据证书中的证据顺序执行一次向前搜索。若某位置上，所有扫描提交经过该证据的前缀修复都必然在此形成词元边界，则将其判定为可靠恢复点。论文通过 committed-prefix lemma 证明该性质，给出完备性与边界安全性之间的严格包含关系及二词元反例，并将算法集成到 munch 词法分析库中，以确定性损坏实验对比传统跳过一个字符和分隔符策略。

Result: 所有证据未被损坏的认证答案均通过可执行落点检查，共 40,885/40,885。对首个答案中证据被损坏触及的 5,595 个案例，有 1,589 个被报告为不确定，未被错误断言。16,808 次试验中，13,522 次被修复程序标记为超出修复能力；在这些案例中，恢复后的后缀可完整词法化，且两种量词条件按证明重合。其余 3,286 次仍无法确定。

Conclusion: 该方法将词法恢复从启发式跳转提升为带证据和形式保证的恢复点选择。其保证是相对于具体自动机和活动模式定义的，并不意味着所有输入都可修复；当证据不可用或损坏影响关键区间时，算法会保持不确定而避免无依据的边界断言。实验结果表明，认证落点具有较强可靠性，但仍需依赖调用者判断证据是否适用于当前干净输入。

Abstract: Classical panic-mode recovery skips a failed scan forward by convention: it promises progress, and membership in a designated set where one is found, but no repair-universal boundary guarantee for the position it lands on. This paper chooses the resume position by theorem: a position is a sound recovery point when every prefix repair whose scan commits through the certificate's returned evidence places a token boundary there, complete repairs the special case, by a committed-prefix lemma over certificates posted for a different purpose (arXiv:2608.03473, arXiv:2608.09761). The quantifier is strictly the stronger one, and a dichotomy locates the difference exactly: the inclusion is strict precisely when no repair completes while some scan still commits through the evidence, a two-token witness realizing the case. The evidence-returning form pairs each answer with the interval it rests on, which is what makes the guarantee deployable: a caller who knows what the scanner cannot check decides whether the certified boundary transfers to the clean input it intended. The search is one forward walk in evidence order and provably terminates; the guarantee is per-automaton, relative to the active mode. The procedure ships in the munch lexing library, and a deterministic corruption study measures its recovery quality beside the classical skip-one and delimiter conventions. Every certified answer whose evidence survives the damage passes its executable landing check on every recovery move, 40,885 of 40,885; among first answers, the 5,595 resting on evidence the damage reached land 1,589 times, reported and never asserted. On 13,522 of the 16,808 trials the shipped repair routine labels beyond repair, the resumed suffix tokenizes whole and the two quantifiers provably coincide, so both are empty there if those negative labels are exact; 3,286 stay undetermined.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

### [79] [Feasible disjunction for random resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10602)
*Theodoros Papamakarios*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明，随机归结的一个更强版本具有可行析取性质。这是首个已知不具备可行插值性质、但具有可行析取性质的证明系统实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可行析取性质与可行插值性质之间的关系，并展示前者并不必然依赖于后者。该结果有助于厘清复杂性受限证明系统中不同结构性质的边界。

Method: 构造并分析随机归结的增强版本，证明对于析取形式的结论，可以从相应证明中有效提取针对各个析取分支的短证明或有效选择。证明重点在于建立该系统的可行析取性质，同时利用已有认知表明其尚不具备可行插值性质。

Result: 证明了增强版随机归结具有可行析取性质，并给出了第一个“未知具有可行插值、但具有可行析取”的证明系统实例。该结果说明可行析取性质的适用范围严格超出已知可行插值框架。

Conclusion: 可行析取性质与可行插值性质并非等价，也不存在后者对前者的必要性。增强版随机归结为研究这两类性质之间的复杂性差异提供了重要案例。

Abstract: We show that a (stronger) version of random resolution has the feasible disjunction property. This is the first instance of a proof system not known to have feasible interpolation, which nevertheless has the feasible disjunction property.

</details>

*Yukiko Kato*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出准闭世界冲突消解图模型（QCW-GMCR），将Belnap四值逻辑、分级可达性、风险态度下的确定性约简、灾难规避均衡及Lean 4形式化验证整合到扩展图模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 形式化验证覆盖经典GMCR稳定性层级、FOUR值合取的代数与组合性质、规范约简算子的性质以及分级可达性层级。验证过程发现并修正了早期理论中的问题：原有知识单调性公理被真值单调性取代。摘要未提供数值实验、案例研究或与其他方法的定量比较。

Conclusion: QCW-GMCR建立了一个能够处理不确定信息、风险偏好、安全约束和主观认知差异的扩展冲突消解框架，并以Lean 4形式化增强了定义和定理的可靠性。当前成果主要是理论体系与部分验证记录，后续仍需进一步扩大形式化覆盖范围，并通过实例或实证案例检验模型的适用性。

Abstract: This note consolidates the core of the Quasi-Closed World Graph Model for Conflict Resolution (QCW-GMCR), which extends the standard Graph Model for Conflict Resolution with Belnap's four-valued logic to represent option-level epistemic ambiguity, and pairs the framework with a machine-checked Lean 4 formalization. QCW-GMCR combines: (1) FOUR-valued option assignments with compositional propagation to state-level feasibility; (2) graded reachability (definite, credible, possible) based on an FDE-inspired transition-warrant semantics, with definite reachability related to FDE consequence in the Boolean fragment; (3) axiomatized deterministic reductions from four-valued assessments to binary decisions, including four canonical operators reflecting distinct risk attitudes; and (4) catastrophe-avoiding equilibrium concepts with a quasi-closed-world safety invariant. A four-valued hypergame extension captures heterogeneous subjective assessments across decision makers. We state the core definitions and results and report the parts verified in Lean 4 with mathlib, including the classical GMCR stability hierarchy, algebraic and compositional properties of FOUR-valued conjunction, properties of the canonical reductions, and the graded reachability hierarchy. The formalization also helped identify and correct earlier claims, including a knowledge-monotonicity axiom replaced by truth monotonicity. This preprint provides a stable, citable record of the framework and its current formal verification status.

</details>


### [81] [The Semantic Elevation Operator and the Closure of the Undecidable Class under Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11326)
*Jose Pascual Gumbau Mezquita*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究自修改系统中的语义性质是否在代码变换后保持不变。作者提出语义提升算子ΛΦ，并声称对于依赖源代码的内涵变换，提升后的性质仍不可判定；反复提升可达到算术层级中的Π⁰₂完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rice定理主要处理程序当前计算函数的外延性质，难以直接描述自修改系统中“性质是否在代码重写后保持”的动态问题。本文旨在建立一套适用于内涵变换和验证器层级的不可判定性分析框架。

Method: 定义语义提升算子ΛΦ，将静态命题“x是否满足性质P”转换为动态命题“经过变换Φ后，P是否仍被保持”。针对依赖源代码而非仅依赖计算函数的内涵变换，作者使用Kleene递归定理证明提升性质的不可判定性，并讨论该算子在非可验证性质类U上的闭包性。进一步通过无界迭代分析其在算术层级中的复杂度，并以递归定理和Lawvere不动点定理提供概念性解释。

Result: 文章声称：即使内涵变换破坏了Rice定理所需的外延性，提升后的性质仍然不可判定；非可验证性质类U在ΛΦ下保持闭合。算子无界迭代后可达到Π⁰₂完备性。作者还声称不存在能够产生无条件证书的有限层级验证器塔，监督式验证回归不会终止。有效拓扑中的范畴化解释被列为未来工作，而非本文已完成的正式结果。

Conclusion: 摘要提出了一个将程序重写保持性与不可判定性联系起来的理论框架，核心贡献是用Kleene递归定理处理非外延的内涵变换，而不是直接依赖Rice定理。不过，Π⁰₂完备性、U类闭包性以及有限验证器塔不可能性等结论需要完整论文中的严格定义、归约构造和复杂度证明支持；摘要本身未说明这些结果成立所需的变换Φ、性质P及可验证性的精确定义。

Abstract: The undecidability of a program's static semantic properties is governed by Rice's theorem. Self-modifying systems, however, require analysing not whether a property holds now, but whether it is preserved when the system rewrites itself. We formalise this transition through a semantic elevation operator ΛΦ, which turns the static question "does x satisfy P?" into the dynamic question "is P preserved after x is transformed by Φ?". We prove that when Φ is intensional (depending on the source code, not only on the computed function), the elevated property remains undecidable even though it breaks the extensionality that Rice's theorem requires; the proof rests on Kleene's recursion theorem, not on Rice. Consequently the class U of non-verifiable properties is closed under the elevation operator. Unbounded iteration of the operator climbs the arithmetical hierarchy -to Π02-completeness- consolidating non-verifiability as a structural fact. We further show that the supervisory regress does not terminate: no fnite tower of increasingly capable verifiers yields an unconditional certificate. A categorical reading of these results in the efective topos, in which elevation appears as an instance of Lawvere's fxed-point theorem, is left as a direction for future work.

</details>


### [82] [Statistical Symmetry Release for Equivariant Quantum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11470)
*Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统计化的对称性释放框架，用于判断何时应放宽硬对称性约束、释放哪些方向以及释放幅度。该框架结合量子测量、局部损失改进、置信界、下界分析和独立验证，将对称性放宽转化为可认证的模型选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬对称性约束能够降低模型复杂度和估计难度，但可能同时删除与标签或任务目标相关的信息。现有方法缺少一种依据有限样本、量子测量结果和任务损失，系统判断是否释放约束及如何选择释放方向的统计原则。本文旨在弥合全局信号检测与局部性能改进之间的联系，并控制方向搜索、模型自适应和参数冗余带来的统计风险。

Method: 作者首先利用双拷贝twirl-swap门，在具有成对状态和群酉访问条件下估计对称性破缺补空间中的任务信息，并证明所需拷贝数与维度无关；通过对相同记录重新加权来分辨不同表示扇区。随后建立精确对偶关系，区分Hilbert-Schmidt信号与有界输出读出可获得的更大信号。局部优化由释放梯度和经损失修正的双对易子矩阵控制。方法采用同时置信界，将经验方向选择转化为具有认证保证的下降步骤，并支持共享Pauli测量或带有状态无关截断界的标量探针。作者还用Gaussian测试下界刻画未知方向搜索的样本代价，通过独立验证控制自适应生成模型，并给出适用于嵌套释放路径的快速平方损失界；训练阶段使用商空间量子自然梯度处理参数冗余。

Result: 在八量子比特Ising模型实验中，共享测量在所测试的预算网格上，相比指定的标量估计器少用约6300倍shots即可认证是否应释放对称性。理论结果覆盖任务信息估计、表示扇区识别、局部下降认证、未知方向搜索下界、自适应模型验证以及嵌套释放路径的近似—估计误差控制。摘要未报告完整的绝对损失提升、释放方向数量、置信水平或不同噪声条件下的性能，因此实验结论主要证明测量效率和决策可认证性。

Conclusion: 本文将对称性约束的放宽从经验性建模选择提升为统计上可检验、可认证的决策流程。核心贡献是把全局对称性破缺信号与任务损失的局部改善联系起来，并通过量子测量设计、置信界和验证机制控制有限样本风险。结果表明，共享测量可显著降低释放决策的采样成本，但方法的适用性仍依赖成对状态、群酉访问、测量实现以及局部实验校准等假设。

Abstract: Hard symmetry constraints reduce model complexity, but can also erase label information. Statistical symmetry release determines when finite data and quantum measurements justify relaxing such a constraint, which directions to open, and how far to move. We connect global signal detection to local, loss-dependent improvement. A two-copy twirl--swap gate estimates task information in the symmetry-breaking complement with a dimension-independent copy count under paired-state and group-unitary access; reweighting the same records resolves representation sectors. An exact duality distinguishes this Hilbert--Schmidt signal from the larger signal accessible to bounded-outcome readouts. Local improvement is governed by the release gradient and a loss-corrected double-commutator matrix. Simultaneous confidence bounds convert empirical direction selection into certified descent, using either shared Pauli measurements or scalar probes with state-independent truncation bounds. Gaussian testing lower bounds quantify the cost of searching over unknown directions in the calibrated local experiment. Independent validation controls adaptively generated models, and a fast squared-loss bound preserves the approximation--estimation rate of a nested release path. On an eight-qubit Ising model, shared measurements certify release with 6300 times fewer shots than the specified scalar estimator on the tested budget grids. Quotient quantum natural gradient then controls parameter redundancy during training. Together, these results turn symmetry relaxation into a statistically justified model-selection decision.

</details>


### [83] [Medvedev Logic is Not Decidable. It is π01 -complete. Who Would Have Guessed?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11576)
*Pawel Pawlowski*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Medvedev 逻辑 ML（有限问题的中间逻辑）在可计算多一归约下是 Π⁰₁-完备的，因此 ML 不可递归枚举、必然不可判定，也不存在递归枚举的可靠且完备证明演算。核心方法是通过 Wang-Medvedev 对，将周期多米诺问题与直觉主义公式的有限 Medvedev 反模型联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究最初旨在借助生成式人工智能证明 Medvedev 逻辑的可判定性，最终却得到相反结论。该问题的重要性在于确定 ML 的计算复杂度与证明论边界，并揭示有限问题逻辑与周期铺砖问题之间的结构性联系。

Method: 作者引入 Wang-Medvedev 对：由有限偏序角色集及其需求组成，需求规定角色间的交互；实现则把角色赋予有限集合的非空子集，同时满足偏序关系和全部需求。首先，为每个有限 Wang 系统构造相应的 Wang-Medvedev 对，并证明该对可实现，当且仅当该系统能够铺满某个有限环面。随后，针对每个这样的对构造直觉主义公式，证明该公式在某个有限 Medvedev 框架上失效，当且仅当对应的 Wang-Medvedev 对可实现。由此把周期多米诺问题归约到 ML 的反例模型存在性问题，再推出 Π⁰₁-完备性。

Result: 论文建立了从周期铺砖问题到 Medvedev 逻辑失效性的有效归约，并证明 ML 在可计算多一归约下是 Π⁰₁-完备的。由 Π⁰₁-完备性可得：ML 不是递归可枚举集，因而不可判定；同时不存在递归枚举、既可靠又完备的证明演算。Wang-Medvedev 对充当了有限铺砖实现与有限 Medvedev 反模型之间的中间结构。

Conclusion: Medvedev 逻辑 ML 的计算复杂度达到 Π⁰₁-完备级别，否定了其可判定性以及递归枚举的可靠完备公理化可能性。论文的主要贡献是提供了一个统一的组合结构，将周期多米诺问题、有限环面铺砖和直觉主义逻辑反模型连接起来；摘要未说明证明中对生成式人工智能系统的具体依赖程度或形式化验证方式。

Abstract: This project began as an attempt to prove that Medvedev logic is decidable with the help of generative AI systems. The author (as well as the generative AI systems, or at least they claim to be since I have asked them) was surprised by its eventual conclusion. We prove that Medvedev logic ML, the intermediate logic of finite problems, is Pi-01-complete under computable many-one reductions. Consequently, ML is not recursively enumerable, a fortiori undecidable, and admits no recursively enumerable sound and complete proof calculus. The proof connects the periodic domino problem with intuitionistic formulas through a shared intermediate structure that we call a Wang-Medvedev pair. Such a pair consists of a finite partially ordered set of roles together with demands. Demands define the interaction between roles. A realization labels nonempty subsets of a finite set with these roles, respecting the order and satisfying the demands. We associate a pair with each finite Wang system and show that it has a realization iff the system tiles a finite torus. We then construct an intuitionistic formula that fails on some finite Medvedev frame iff the same pair is realizable. Realizability thus provides the link between periodic tilings and the countermodels.

</details>


### [84] [Warrant Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11667)
*Khashayar Irani*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文提出“凭证理论”作为一种哲学化的逻辑理论，将逻辑理解为关于命题何时可以被合法接受、拒绝及用于推理的规范性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逻辑常以真值、信念或形式有效性为核心，而本文试图转向“推理资格”这一更基础的问题：一个命题凭什么能够进入推理、承担承诺并支持后续结论。

Method: 文章通过区分凭证与真、信念等心理或语义概念，建立一套凭证理论分析框架；该框架考察命题如何获得推理地位，以及不同推理地位之间的依赖、兼容、不兼容和排斥关系，并以接受与拒绝构成双向表述体系。

Result: 论文形成了一种凭证理论式的逻辑定义：逻辑是对命题可被合法接受或拒绝的条件，以及由这种合法性所授权的推理转换进行形式化和规范性研究。逻辑后承与逻辑失败分别被解释为推理资格的存在、保持或缺失。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于把逻辑的哲学主题从单纯的真值保持或形式关系，重新定位为对推理合法性的系统治理；其理论价值在于为接受、拒绝、承诺和推理后果提供统一的规范性说明框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop warrant theory as a philosophical discipline concerned with the inferential legitimacy of propositions within logical analysis. Warrant theory reconceptualises logic as a normative framework governing the conditions under which propositions may be introduced, accepted, rejected, and inferentially employed. Warrant is understood as inferential entitlement and is distinguished from truth, belief, and other psychological attitudes, while its relation to inferential use and meaning is examined. Warrant-theoretic analysis is then developed as a systematic method for investigating how propositions acquire inferential standing, how that standing develops, and how inferential positions interact through relations of dependence, compatibility, incompatibility, and exclusion. Acceptance and rejection provide the bilateral vocabulary for representing positive and negative inferential positions and the consequences and commitments associated with them. Finally, these elements are brought together in a warrant-theoretic definition of logic as the formal and normative study of the conditions under which propositions may be legitimately accepted or rejected and of the inferential transitions that such legitimacy warrants. On this account, logical consequence and logical failure are understood through the presence, preservation, or absence of inferential entitlement, thus locating the philosophical subject matter of logic in the systematic governance of inferential legitimacy.

</details>


### [85] [A Lumpability-Driven Taxonomy of Strong and Weak Stochastic Bisimilarities with Their Congruence Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11893)
*Riccardo Romanello,Andrea Esposito,Marco Bernardo,Carla Piazza,Sabina Rossi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文系统梳理了 PEPA 中基于 CTMC lumpability 的随机双模拟等价关系，统一命名并建立六类强/弱双模拟的包含关系、特殊情形下的变化及其组合性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEPA 过程项可对应到连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC），而 lumpability 能通过状态聚合降低分析复杂度。但已有随机双模拟概念分散在文献中，命名不一致甚至冲突，导致不同等价关系与其诱导的 lumping 类型之间的关系不够清晰。

Method: 论文在统一框架下重新定义并命名三类双模拟：ordinary、exact、strict，并分别给出强、弱两个版本。随后证明这些双模拟分别诱导 ordinary、exact、strict lumping，建立六种双模拟之间“所有且仅有”的包含关系，并进一步考察时间可逆 CTMC、无不可观测活动、无递归过程项三种特殊场景。最后分析这些等价关系在 PEPA 前缀和选择算子下的同余性。

Result: 核心结果包括：六种随机双模拟被组织成完整分类体系；不同双模拟与不同 lumpability 概念之间的对应关系被明确化；在若干受限过程类中，原有包含关系可能简化或发生变化；部分双模拟不是 PEPA 前缀或选择算子的同余关系，论文因此给出可保持同余性的过程项集合，或给出包含于相应双模拟中的最粗同余关系。

Conclusion: 该工作主要贡献是概念整合与理论澄清：它为 PEPA 中随机双模拟、CTMC 聚合和组合性之间的关系提供了统一分类与严格证明，有助于后续在性能模型约简、语义比较和组合式建模中更准确地选择合适的等价关系。

Abstract: We study the relationships among the stochastic bisimulation-style equivalences over PEPA - Performance Evaluation Process Algebra definable according to the well known notions of lumpability for the continuous-time Markov chains (CTMCs) underlying process terms. Lumpability is a central tool in the analysis of a CTMC, because it results in aggregations of the state space enjoying properties that are useful for efficiently computing the state probability distribution of the original chain. At the level of process terms, various stochastic bisimilarities accounting for activity types and cumulative rates can be defined over PEPA, which induce different kinds of lumping. Since the formalisations of some of them are scattered across the literature, where they appear under different, and sometimes clashing, names, we collect them within a single, uniform framework, renaming each bisimilarity in a consistent way after the kind of lumping it induces. We present strong and weak variants of what we call ordinary, exact, and strict bisimilarities and show that they respectively induce ordinary, exact, and strict lumpings. We then organise the six bisimilarities into a taxonomy establishing all and only the inclusions holding among them. We also analyse how the taxonomy changes in three special cases: process terms whose underlying CTMCs are time reversible, process terms with no activities of unobservable types, and process terms with no recursion. The paper concludes by investigating the compositionality properties of the six bisimilarities. Some of them are not congruences with respect to the prefix and/or choice operators of PEPA. In that case we single out either a set of process terms over which congruence with respect to those operators is achieved, or the coarsest congruence with respect to them that is contained in the considered bisimilarity.

</details>

### [86] [An Empirical Measurement of Jailbreaking Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10594)
*Yujie Mu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种自动化越狱评估器，并以人工标注为参考，发现JADES总体表现最佳，HarmBench和StrongReject也具有较好的评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工评估越狱响应成本高且难以扩展，而不同研究往往独立验证评估器，造成资源重复消耗和结果难以比较。此外，不同评估器对越狱成功的定义存在差异，可能显著影响攻击强度和研究进展的判断。因此，有必要在统一数据和受控条件下系统比较常用评估器。

Method: 研究选取HarmBench、JailbreakBench、JailbreakRadar、StrongReject、JADES和JailMeter六种评估器，在JailbreakQR和JailMeter-Eva两个具有人类判断标注的数据集上进行测试。以人工判断作为参考标准，测量各评估器与人类判断的一致性、错误类型及其在不同攻击家族上的稳定性。对于依赖通用大语言模型作为裁判的评估器，统一使用同一模型骨干，以控制模型差异带来的影响。

Result: JADES取得整体最佳性能；HarmBench和StrongReject同样表现良好。研究还从人类一致性、错误模式和跨攻击家族一致性等维度，对六种评估器进行了系统比较。

Conclusion: 自动化评估器的选择会影响越狱研究中对攻击成功率和防御效果的判断。JADES在统一受控实验中显示出较强的综合可靠性，而HarmBench和StrongReject也是具有竞争力的选择。未来研究应采用统一基准和明确的人类参考标准，以提高不同论文之间结果的可比性。

Abstract: Expert evaluation of jailbreak responses is costly and difficult to scale, so the community increasingly relies on automated evaluators to determine whether an attack succeeds. However, jailbreak studies typically validate their chosen evaluator independently, repeatedly spending resources on similar evaluation efforts while making results across papers difficult to compare. Different evaluators also encode different definitions of jailbreak success, meaning that reported attack strength and apparent progress can depend substantially on which evaluator is used. We systematically compare six evaluators that recur in recent jailbreak attack and defense research: HarmBench, JailbreakBench, JailbreakRadar, StrongReject, JADES, and JailMeter. To our knowledge, no prior study has evaluated all six on the same human-labeled data under a controlled setup. We evaluate them on JailbreakQR and JailMeter-Eva, using human judgments as the reference, and measure agreement with humans, error types, and consistency across attack families. For evaluators that require a general-purpose LLM judge, we use a shared backbone to control for model-specific variation. We found that JADES exhibits the best overall performance, while HarmBench and StrongReject also demonstrate good performance.

</details>


### [87] [Threshold Choice, Not Sample Size, Bounds Trustless Verification of Nondeterministic Compound AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10601)
*Alper Alimoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向复合AI流水线的可复现性验证协议，试图同时处理输出非确定性、执行节点不诚实和共享记录间歇不可用三类问题。实验表明，协议能较好识别部分差异执行，但固定阈值无法阻止同输入伪造；增加重执行次数也不能解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI流水线由大语言模型调用、检索器和工具组成，受随机采样、模型更新及动态工具响应影响，同一输入可能产生不同结果。现有验证方法通常只能处理其中部分挑战，且在边缘、云端及轨道节点等多方协作环境中，还需考虑执行节点不可信和记录访问中断。

Method: 协议为每个阶段的输入、输出和上下文提交摘要，并通过策略摘要固定评估指标与阈值；提交结果在不信任执行节点的情况下进行锚定，网络分区时延后处理。验证阶段采用无须法定人数的机制，并以k次重执行结果的中位数决定是否提出挑战。研究进一步比较固定阈值与一种按每次执行推导的判定规则。

Result: 在合成HotpotQA流水线上，校准后的固定阈值接受了45次诚实复现中的44次，并拒绝105组发散结果中的104组，但在29次同输入伪造试验中有27次被放行。增加k值不能消除该问题，因为更多样本只会提高估计稳定性而不会改变系统性偏差。在相同指标和重执行离散度下，阈值是主要约束：按执行推导的规则在29次试验中识别19次，而保持同样零诚实拒绝率的最佳固定阈值仅识别9次；该规则在k=5时未拒绝诚实提交，在k=3时拒绝15次中的3次，并识别出针对该规则构造的15次攻击中的11次。该规则目前仅完成测量评估，尚未部署。

Conclusion: 研究的核心结论是，面对具有系统性偏差的复合AI流水线，单纯增加重执行次数或依赖固定阈值不足以保证复现验证。判定规则的设计和阈值选择比样本数量更关键；按执行情况动态推导的指标显示出更强的伪造识别能力，但仍存在误拒绝和适应性攻击风险，尚不能视为已验证的部署方案。

Abstract: Compound AI pipelines chain LLM calls, retrievers, and tools and are nondeterministic: sampling, model updates, and volatile tool responses make one input yield different outputs across runs. Such pipelines increasingly run across edge, cloud, and orbital nodes owned by no single party, whose optimizations discard intermediate results before inspection. Verifying reproduction there means tolerating nondeterministic outputs, a node that may not report honestly, and intermittent access to any shared record; existing work addresses at most two at once. We give a protocol covering all three: it commits digests of each stage's inputs, outputs, and context under a policy digest pinning the metric and threshold, anchors them without trusting the executing node, defers under partition, and decides a challenge on the median of $k$ re-executions, with no quorum. Where that procedure breaks is the main result. On a synthetic HotpotQA pipeline a calibrated fixed threshold accepts 44 of 45 honest reproductions and rejects 104 of 105 divergent pairs, yet lets same-input fabrication through in 27 of 29 trials at k=5, more samples being no help since sampling sharpens an estimate without moving it. Holding that metric and this pipeline's re-execution spread fixed, the binding constraint is the threshold rather than the sample size: one derived per execution detects 19 of 29 where the best constant matched to the same zero honest rejections reaches 9, rejects no honest commitment at k=5 though 3 of 15 at k=3, and catches 11 of 15 of an attacker built against it, which has to aim at a target drawn only after its commitment exists. That rule is measured rather than deployed.

</details>


### [88] [Adaptive Diffusion Freezing: Privacy-preserving Diffusion Models Against Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10608)
*Jialu Guo,Xiao Han,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应扩散冻结（ADF）框架，通过跨时间步自适应冻结训练降低扩散模型在成员推断攻击下的隐私泄露，并在隐私、生成质量与训练效率之间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型训练可能过度记忆成员样本，从而暴露成员关系。现有隐私保护方法难以同时兼顾防御效果、生成效用和计算效率，因此需要一种能够针对不同数据子集与扩散时间步进行精细控制的方法。

Method: ADF使用掩码矩阵控制不同数据子集在各扩散时间步中的训练参与程度。该矩阵由基于预训练模型的风险感知冻结策略构建，通过估计样本的记忆倾向和成员推断风险，抑制高风险的子集—时间步组合，减少过度记忆并使成员与非成员样本产生更一致的模型行为。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，ADF能够有效降低成员推断风险，并相较多种基线方法取得具有竞争力的隐私保护效果，以及更优的隐私—效用—效率综合权衡。摘要未提供具体攻击指标、生成质量数值或训练加速比例。

Conclusion: ADF通过跨时间步、跨数据子集的风险感知冻结机制，为扩散模型隐私保护提供了较细粒度的训练控制方案。其主要贡献在于改善隐私、生成质量和效率之间的平衡，但方法的泛化能力及具体收益仍需结合完整实验结果进一步验证。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generative tasks across various areas, however their training process raises significant privacy concerns, particularly under membership inference attacks (MIAs). Prior studies on privacy-preserving of diffusion models fail to balance privacy, utility, and efficiency. To address this gap, we propose a novel framework of privacy-preserving diffusion models, Adaptive Diffusion Freezing (ADF), which can defend against MIAs with better trade-off. By leveraging cross-timestep adaptive freezing training, ADF explicitly control the participation of different data subsets across diffusion timesteps via a mask matrix, which reduces the over-memorization and leads to more uniform model behaviors between member and nonmember samples. To construct a freezing mask matrix that effectively reduce membership leakage without unnecessarily harming generation quality, we introduce a pretraining-based risk-aware freezing policy to estimate MIA risk based on memorization tendency, and suppress the contribution of the subset-timestep pairs with higher risk. Evaluations on multiple datasets demonstrate that ADF provides effective defense performance as well as state-of-the-art privacy-utility-efficiency trade-off performance compared to various baselines.

</details>


### [89] [Black-Box Membership Inference via Word-Level Probability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10611)
*Shengjie Niu,Yeheng Ge,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出WPMIA，一种面向严格黑盒大语言模型的成员推断攻击方法。它仅依赖模型生成的文本续写，通过蒙特卡洛采样和局部核平滑估计词级生成概率，再构造序列级似然并利用不同前缀增强成员与非成员之间的差异。在多种开源及专有模型上均优于现有黑盒基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推断攻击通常需要访问逐词logits或概率，这使其无法用于仅提供文本生成接口的专有大语言模型。论文旨在建立一种不依赖内部概率信息、可用于严格黑盒隐私审计的成员推断方法。

Method: WPMIA首先通过蒙特卡洛采样获取文本续写，并结合局部核平滑估计目标词的生成概率；随后将词级概率聚合为序列级似然估计。方法进一步在不同文本前缀条件下计算似然，以放大训练成员与非成员在生成分布上的差异，并据此完成成员判别。

Result: 在多种开源大语言模型上的实验表明，WPMIA持续优于已有黑盒成员推断基线。在GPT-5-Chat、Gemini-2.5-Flash和Claude-4.5-Haiku等专有模型上，平均TPR@5%FPR达到42.0，说明该方法在严格黑盒场景下具有较强的实际审计能力。摘要未报告各模型的单独结果、置信区间或计算开销。

Conclusion: WPMIA为无法访问内部概率信息的专有大语言模型提供了具有统计依据的成员推断方案，证明仅凭文本续写也能有效开展训练数据隐私审计。其主要贡献在于将概率估计、序列似然聚合和多前缀条件建模结合起来，但方法的泛化性、采样成本及对采样接口限制的敏感性仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) have emerged as critical tools for auditing privacy risks in large language models (LLMs), aiming to determine whether a given text was included in a model's training corpus. However, most existing MIAs require access to per-token logits or probabilities, making them inapplicable in practice to proprietary LLMs that expose only textual continuations. To address this underexplored setting, we propose Word-level Probability MIA (WPMIA), a statistically principled MIA for strict black-box privacy auditing. WPMIA estimates word-level generation probabilities via Monte Carlo sampling with local kernel smoothing, then aggregates these estimates into a sequence-level likelihood estimator. Furthermore, WPMIA constructs the likelihood conditioned on different prefixes, thereby amplifying the distributional differences between members and non-members. We evaluate WPMIA across various open-source LLMs and find that it consistently outperforms existing black-box baselines. Importantly, we also evaluate WPMIA on modern proprietary LLMs, including GPT-5-Chat, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and Claude-4.5-Haiku, achieving an average TPR@5\%FPR of 42.0 across these models. These results offer a sound foundation for future research on strict black-box membership inference. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}.

</details>


### [90] [PEARL: A Task-Aware Framework for Evaluating Differentially Private Synthetic Educational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10612)
*Xianghui Meng,Yujing Zhang,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PEARL审计框架，用于只有在同时满足数据有效性、差分隐私、预测有用性、公平性及特定教育任务适用性时，才批准发布差分隐私合成学习者数据。96种实验设置中仅12种通过全部适用检查，表明隐私保护和一般预测性能并不等同于个性化学习任务可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化学习系统需要使用学习者的表现、行为和人口统计数据，但这些数据具有高度隐私敏感性。现有研究通常将隐私保护与预测效用分开评估，无法判断合成数据是否保留了面向辍学预测、知识追踪和自适应辅导等具体任务所需的信息，也难以识别数据遗漏关键群体或造成群体间性能不平等的问题。

Method: 提出PEARL（Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger），对每个差分隐私合成数据集执行有效性、隐私保护、预测有用性、任务适用性和公平性检查，并记录未通过数据集的具体失败原因。研究在96种设置下进行比较，每种设置由数据集、数据生成方法、隐私预算和随机种子共同定义；同时检验学习活动顺序、结果群体保留情况、不同群体的高风险预测性能，以及Deep Knowledge Tracing和Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing对下一响应模式的学习能力。

Result: 96种设置中只有12种合成数据集通过全部适用的PEARL检查。部分数据集虽然满足隐私要求，却遗漏了退学学生等重要结果群体，或未保留学习活动的时间顺序。公平性分析发现，一些同时通过隐私和一般预测效用检查的数据集，在残障和社会经济背景群体之间仍产生不平等的高风险预测性能。所有测试的合成知识追踪数据集都未能使Deep Knowledge Tracing和Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing学习到有意义的下一响应模式。

Conclusion: 差分隐私能够降低个体学习者的数据暴露风险，但不能保证合成数据适用于具体的个性化教育任务。PEARL强调应采用面向任务、包含公平性和结构保真度检查的综合发布标准，并透明记录拒绝原因。研究结果表明，合成数据发布前必须验证关键群体、行为时序和任务相关预测信号是否得到保留。

Abstract: Personalized learning systems rely on real learner data, including performance, behavior, and demographic information, but these data are highly privacy-sensitive. Differentially private (DP) synthetic data can support system development and educational research while reducing exposure of individual learners. Existing evaluations, however, assess privacy and predictive usefulness separately, without determining whether synthetic learner data remain usable for the intended personalized learning task. We introduce PEARL (Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger), which approves a DP synthetic educational dataset only when it passes all required checks of validity, privacy protection, predictive usefulness, and suitability for the intended educational task, while recording why each rejected dataset fails. Across 96 study settings, each defined by a dataset, data-generation method, privacy budget, and random seed, only 12 produced synthetic datasets that passed all applicable PEARL checks. Many privacy-protected datasets were rejected for omitting important outcome groups, such as withdrawn students, or for failing to preserve the order of learning activities. Fairness analysis further showed that some datasets passing privacy and predictive-usefulness checks still yielded unequal at-risk prediction performance across groups defined by disability and socioeconomic background. Moreover, Deep Knowledge Tracing and Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing learned no meaningful next-response patterns from any tested synthetic knowledge-tracing dataset, showing that privacy protection alone does not guarantee usefulness for dropout prediction, knowledge tracing, or adaptive tutoring.

</details>


### [91] [Understanding In-Context Multimodal Jailbreaks via Posterior Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10613)
*Xu Zhang,Dev Mistry,Xiang Xu,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出后验重加权框架，将多模态大语言模型中的上下文学习越狱解释为示范证据累积导致的安全与有害行为后验偏移，并据此设计自适应防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已观察到有害示范能够在不修改模型参数的情况下诱导模型产生不安全输出，但尚缺乏统一机制解释其成功原因，以及示范数量、比例、对抗强度和语义多样性如何影响攻击效果。

Method: 将安全对齐的多模态大语言模型建模为具有竞争性行为模式的系统，把上下文示范视为推理时证据，并通过后验重加权刻画安全偏好向有害偏好的动态转移。在此基础上，根据风险估计自适应注入良性反证据，以抑制有害后验漂移。

Result: 该框架给出了关于示范数量、有害示范比例、对抗强度和语义多样性的预测性扩展规律。实验表明，在固定干预预算下，所提出方法相较现有上下文防御方法取得了更优的鲁棒性—效用权衡。

Conclusion: 后验重加权能够统一解释并预测多模态大语言模型中的上下文学习越狱现象，同时为低成本、自适应的推理时防御提供理论依据和有效方案。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) jailbreaks reveal a critical vulnerability in multimodal large language models (MLLMs): harmful demonstrations in the prompt can induce unsafe outputs without modifying model parameters. Despite extensive empirical evidence, existing work lacks a principled understanding of why such jailbreaks reliably succeed or how their effectiveness scales with context composition. We propose a posterior reweighting framework that models a safety-aligned MLLM as implicitly operating over competing behavioral modes, and interprets in-context demonstrations as inference-time evidence that dynamically shifts the model's posterior preference between safe and harmful behaviors. This view formalizes jailbreak as a process of evidence accumulation, yielding predictive scaling laws with respect to demonstration count, harmful ratio, adversarial strength, and semantic diversity. Guided by this framework, we introduce a posterior-aware inference-time defense that adaptively injects benign counter-evidence based on estimated risk, effectively suppressing harmful posterior drift while preserving model utility. Compared to existing in-context defenses, our method achieves a significantly improved robustness-utility trade-off under a fixed intervention budget. Together, our results establish posterior reweighting as a unifying and predictive framework for understanding and mitigating ICL jailbreak in MLLMs.

</details>


### [92] [SoK: Privacy Attacks on Machine Learning via Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10627)
*Abdullah Caglar Oksuz,Anisa Halimi,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了25项利用机器学习解释进行模型提取、成员推断、模型反演及部分属性推断的研究，提出应从解释信号的获取路径与攻击目标出发评估解释隐私风险，而不能仅依据黑盒或白盒标签判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习解释不仅呈现预测结果，还可能泄露模型参数、训练集成员关系及输入数据。现有研究对攻击者掌握的模型知识与解释获取方式区分不足，导致威胁模型描述不完整，也难以准确比较不同攻击和防御方法。

Method: 作者对25项相关研究进行系统化比较，区分模型知识与解释获取方式，并提出五类获取路径：目标模型直接发布、攻击者自行推导、二次披露、特权访问和公开的全局解释产物。同时从解释信号、辅助知识、目标模型、数据模态、查询预算、评价指标、攻击性能及防御措施等维度分析研究结果，并将属性推断视为部分模型反演问题。

Result: 研究发现，解释可降低模型提取成本；解释统计量、反事实式复原距离及解释引导的鲁棒性分析能够增强成员推断；空间重建或代数重构则可恢复私有输入。不同解释类型的风险并不一致，防御效果也缺乏普适性，实际风险取决于暴露的信号、信号获取路径、被保护资产以及攻击者已有知识。

Conclusion: 解释隐私应被视为端到端的信息披露问题，而非简单的黑盒或白盒安全问题。防御设计应针对具体的解释获取路径和保护目标，并通过统一、细化的威胁模型评估解释对模型机密性和数据隐私的影响。

Abstract: Machine learning explanations reveal model behavior beyond predictions, creating attack surfaces for model confidentiality and data privacy. We systematize 25 studies that exploit explanations for model extraction, membership inference, and model inversion, treating attribute inference as partial inversion. Existing work is often labeled only black- or white-box, obscuring substantial differences in what explanation signal reaches an adversary. We therefore separate model knowledge from explanation acquisition and identify five paths: target-released, attacker-derived, secondary disclosure, privileged access, and released global artifacts. Across these paths, explanations reduce extraction cost, expose membership signals through explanation statistics, recourse distance, and explanation-guided robustness, and support spatial or algebraic reconstruction of private inputs. We compare system and threat models, explanation signals, auxiliary knowledge, target models, modalities, query budgets, evaluation metrics, reported performance, and defenses. Our analysis shows that no explanation family is uniformly unsafe and no defense is uniformly effective. Risk depends on which signal is exposed, how it is acquired, which asset is targeted, and what the attacker already knows. We argue that explanation privacy should therefore be evaluated as an end-to-end disclosure problem, with defenses matched to the acquisition path and protected asset.

</details>


### [93] [From Cycle Space to Cycle Manifold: Limits and Achievability of Blind False Data Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10631)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明，在连通直流支路潮流模型下，基于无噪声正交检验的残差敏感子空间恰好等于加权环空间，其正交补构成完整的隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击空间；同时给出测量驱动的攻击空间重构方法，并将结论扩展至交流模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有盲攻击方法通常仅从代数角度学习低秩测量子空间，未明确揭示攻击隐蔽性所依赖的物理电网约束，也未说明恢复完整攻击空间所需的最少信息。本文旨在建立加权环空间与隐蔽攻击空间之间的严格联系，并分析网络拓扑及支路参数的可辨识性。

Method: 首先基于连通直流支路潮流模型，推导无噪声正交检验中残差敏感子空间与加权环空间的等价关系，并通过正交补刻画完整隐蔽攻击空间。随后分析该空间对网络拓扑和相对环边参数的辨识能力，指出拓扑只能辨识到2-同构，双连通分量内参数只能辨识到一个尺度因子，而桥支路参数既无法辨识也不影响攻击空间。研究进一步构建计算上不受约束的基准方法和仅依赖测量的可行重构方法。对于交流模型，则利用支路有功/无功测量的环流形进行拓扑辅助拟合与GPU测量生成，并研究无损、定电压、小角度极限。

Result: 理论结果表明，加权环空间知识对于完整盲式虚假数据注入攻击既是必要条件也是充分条件。实验在IEEE测试系统上，以95%名义接受率阈值比较检测器绕过率与状态影响，验证了所提空间刻画和重构方法的有效性。交流扩展显示，支路P/Q测量可由环流形描述，并可通过拓扑辅助拟合和GPU加速生成测量；在无损定电压小角度条件下，交流有功测量切片的法空间退化为直流加权环空间。

Conclusion: 本文将隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击从抽象低秩代数问题转化为具有明确物理含义的加权环空间问题，统一解释了完整攻击空间、信息需求和参数不可辨识性。结果表明，桥支路无需恢复，而双连通结构及其相对参数决定了主要攻击自由度；交流模型在特定近似下与直流结论一致，为从直流理论向交流测量生成和攻击建模扩展提供了基础。

Abstract: A false data injection attack (FDIA) can change the estimated grid state while evading a residual-based bad data detector (BDD). Existing blind attacks learn a low-rank measurement subspace, but this algebraic view does not state the physical grid constraints that make an attack stealthy or the minimum information needed to recover the complete attack space. Under the connected direct-current (DC) branch-flow model, we show that the residual-sensitive subspace of the noiseless orthogonal test is exactly the weighted cycle space. Its orthogonal complement is therefore the complete stealthy attack space, making weighted cycle-space knowledge both necessary and sufficient for complete blind FDIA. This space identifies the topology only up to 2-isomorphism and the relative cycle-edge parameters only up to one scale per biconnected component; bridge parameters are neither identified nor required. We then formulate a computationally unconstrained benchmark and a tractable measurement-only reconstruction method. Experiments on IEEE systems compare BDD bypass rate at a 95% nominal-acceptance threshold against state impact. As a compact alternating-current (AC) extension, we characterize feasible branch P/Q measurements by a cycle manifold and demonstrate topology-assisted manifold fitting and measurement generation on a graphics processing unit (GPU). In the lossless fixed-voltage small-angle limit, the normal space of the active-power slice reduces to the DC weighted cycle space.

</details>


### [94] [HermiCache: Enclave-Aware Cache Replacement for Trusted Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10634)
*Oussama Elmnaouri,Pascal Cotret,Vianney Lapôtre,Loïc Lagadec*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HermiCache，一种面向RISC-V可信执行环境的确定性、细粒度缓存侧信道防护机制，并在CVA6处理器和Keystone TEE上实现，核心面积开销为6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE虽然能够隔离受保护内存与不可信软件，但缓存等共享微架构资源仍会泄露信息。现有缓存分区和随机化方案难以同时满足细粒度配置与确定性防护需求。

Method: 设计HermiCache缓存防护机制，针对RISC-V核心进行硬件集成，并在OpenHwGroup CVA6处理器核心中实现；软件层结合Keystone TEE进行验证。

Result: HermiCache已完成硬件与软件协同实现，实验平台采用CVA6和Keystone。论文摘要明确报告了处理器核心面积开销为6%，但未给出具体安全性、性能或能耗提升数据。

Conclusion: HermiCache为RISC-V TEE提供了面向缓存侧信道攻击的可配置且确定性的防护方案，在较低的核心面积开销下实现了原型集成；其实际防护强度和运行时性能仍需结合完整实验结果评估。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) protect enclave memory from untrusted software but remain vulnerable to cache-based side-channel attacks due to shared microarchitectural resources. Existing countermeasures use techniques such as cache partitioning or randomization: these solutions are not ideal if a designer wants fine-grained configurations and a deterministic protection. In this paper, we introduce HermiCache which is an answer to these requirements. HermiCache is designed for RISC-V cores and has been implemented in the OpenHwGroup CVA6 core with a Keystone TEE for the software layer. The solution has an area overhead of 6% on the processor core.

</details>


### [95] [Architecting the Secure AI-SOC: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Pipeline Integrity and Threat Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10707)
*Anna Gazani,Spyridon Kounoupidis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Nikolaos Kekatos,Grigorios Tsoumakas,Georgios Koutidis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向安全运营中心的神经符号纵深防御架构，用于缓解通过日志投毒实施的间接提示注入，并在保证处理效率的同时提升语义安全性、可观测性和端到端完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型用于安全运营中心能够提升威胁情报处理能力，但日志内容可能被恶意构造并作为间接提示注入载荷，进而影响模型的分析逻辑。现有确定性防御方法效率高但缺乏语义理解，纯神经评估方法则存在延迟高、结果不稳定等问题，因此需要融合规则约束与语义验证的防御方案。

Method: 架构包含三部分：第一层使用定制化SIEM解码器作为确定性预过滤器，在数据摄入边缘执行结构清洗，削弱容量填充和基于特征的注入；第二层使用NeMo Guardrails，对结构化SIEM告警执行自检式语义验证，在进入LLM前施加语义边界；第三层构建闭环遥测系统，将被阻断攻击的相关信息呈现在SOC仪表板中，为人工介入提供可见性。实验依据MITRE ATLAS分类体系，使用多样化提示注入场景评估整体防御能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，确定性预过滤与语义护栏能够协同削弱由日志投毒驱动的多步骤提示注入链条，并通过可验证约束降低LLM随机性。闭环遥测机制进一步提升了攻击阻断过程的可观测性和人工审查能力。摘要未报告具体数据集、基线、攻击成功率、误报率、延迟或统计显著性，因此定量性能优势仍无法独立核验。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了神经符号纵深防御在AI赋能SOC中的应用潜力：规则层负责低延迟结构防护，语义层负责上下文约束，遥测层负责审计与人工监督。其主要贡献是形成端到端、可观测的防护流程，但仍需通过更详细的实验设置、公开基线和量化指标验证泛化能力、性能开销及对自适应攻击的稳健性。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Security Operations Centers (SOCs) streamlines threat intelligence but introduces critical vulnerabilities, notably indirect prompt injection via log poisoning. Adversaries exploit this vector to execute multistep ``promptware'' kill chains by embedding malicious payloads within system logs to hijack the LLM's operational logic.
