Securing this pipeline presents a dichotomy: deterministic defenses are computationally efficient yet semantically blind, while purely neural evaluations introduce prohibitive latency and probabilistic flaws. To address this, we propose a novel neurosymbolic defense-in-depth architecture that ensures end-to-end pipeline integrity. The primary layer employs customized SIEM decoders as a deterministic pre-filter, performing immediate structural sanitization to neutralize volumetric padding and signature-based injections at the ingestion edge. The secondary layer leverages NeMo Guardrails to enforce strict semantic boundaries through self-checking validation on the structured SIEM alerts prior to LLM processing. Furthermore, the framework integrates a closed-loop telemetry system, providing critical Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) visibility into thwarted attacks directly within the SOC dashboard. We present a comprehensive experimental evaluation mapped to the MITRE ATLAS taxonomy, assessing the framework against diverse prompt injections. Our results demonstrate that this synergistic approach effectively dismantles the promptware kill chain - bounding LLM stochasticity with verifiable constraints, and delivering a resilient, highly observable defense mechanism for next-generation AI-SOCs.

</details>


### [96] [CARTS: Contextual Autoregressive Rank Transcoding Steganography for Full-Capacity Keyed Text Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10744)
*Wissam Ghantous,Alexander V. Mantzaris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文将CARTS形式化为一种基于自回归语言模型、保持逐位置rank信息的等长隐写转码方法，并首次给出 correctness 与若干安全性质的理论分析及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有Calgacus构造已展示语言模型可用于将payload文本转成等token长度的stegotext，但缺少严格的安全定义、正确性证明和对潜在攻击面的系统分析，因此本文试图为该方向建立形式化基础。

Method: 作者在确定性模型假设下证明精确正确性；提出rank-coordinate表示，将密钥建模为rank向量空间上的双射；定义并分析context search、key collision、message equivocation、encoding map non-commutativity等问题及其关系；并在Llama 3 8B上进行实证测试。

Result: 理论上，论文刻画了message equivocation与context search之间的关系，并指出key collision与message equivocation之间存在张力。实验上，所有测试均能精确恢复payload，随机密钥未发现碰撞，手工构造的碰撞仅具局部性，且未发现可交换密钥对。

Conclusion: 该工作主要贡献在于把经验性语言模型隐写构造推进到可证明分析框架中，增强了CARTS作为隐私通信工具的理论可信度；但结论依赖确定性模型设定和有限实验，后续仍需扩展到随机采样、真实威胁模型和更大规模攻击验证。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models can be used to transform a payload text into a stegotext of identical token length by preserving per-position rank information across contexts - a methodology we formalize as Contextual Autoregressive Rank Transcoding Steganography (CARTS). While the Calgacus construction of Norelli et al. demonstrated this phenomenon experimentally, no formal security analysis existed. This paper provides the first rigorous treatment of CARTS. We show its exact correctness under deterministic model assumptions, introduce a rank-coordinate representation in which keys act as bijections on rank-vector space, define relevant security notions and the computational problems naturally associated with the construction - context search, key collisions, message equivocation, and non-commutativity of the encoding maps - and study the theoretical relationships between them, including the characterization of message equivocation in terms of context search, and the tension between key collisions and message equivocation. An empirical study on Llama 3 8B confirms exact recovery of the original payload in all tested cases, finds no key collisions under random key generation, establishes that a hand-crafted collision is local rather than global, and finds no commuting key pairs - suggesting resistance to the attack vectors studied. This work opens a formally grounded research agenda for the constructive use of language models in cryptography and privacy-preserving communication.

</details>


### [97] [Temporal and Multimodal Deep Learning for Cyberattack Detection in LEO Satellite Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10746)
*Kyle Stein,Guillermo Francia,Eman El-Sheikh,Hossain Shahriar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究面向LEO卫星通信系统的网络威胁检测，基于卫星专用UNSW-IoTSAT数据集系统评估深度学习模型，重点证明结构化多模态建模和防数据泄漏评估协议的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常依赖地面网络入侵数据集，或将观测样本独立处理，难以反映LEO卫星中RF链路、星载硬件与轨道运行等异构信息，也不足以捕捉卫星场景中的时间演化模式。

Method: 论文比较了保留子系统结构的模型架构，包括Subsystem-Fusion MLP和层级多模态Transformer；后者同时建模跨子系统交互与时序动态。实验还覆盖行级划分、时间划分和跨卫星泛化等更严格的评估协议。

Result: 实验显示，结构化多模态模型优于更简单的独立观测建模方式；在防泄漏评估协议下，层级Transformer最高达到91.66%准确率和85.63%宏平均F1，说明其在类别不均衡或多类型检测场景下具有较强稳健性。

Conclusion: 该摘要的主要贡献在于将卫星特定数据、子系统级信息融合、时序建模和严格评估结合起来，为LEO卫星网络威胁检测提供了更可信的实验框架。潜在不足是摘要未说明数据规模、类别分布、基线模型细节和跨卫星泛化的具体性能差距。

Abstract: The growing reliance on Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite communication systems has increased the need for intelligent methods capable of detecting cyberattacks across complex and dynamic space environments. Unlike conventional network intrusion detection, satellite systems generate heterogeneous information across radio-frequency (RF) links, onboard hardware, and orbital operations. However, many existing approaches either rely on terrestrial intrusion datasets or evaluate individual observations independently, limiting their ability to capture temporal attack behavior specific to LEO satellites. In this work, we conduct a systematic study of deep-learning-based cyberattack detection using the recently introduced satellite-specific UNSW-IoTSAT dataset. We investigate structured learning architectures that preserve hardware, orbital, and RF information, including a Subsystem-Fusion MLP and a hierarchical multimodal Transformer that models both cross-subsystem interactions and temporal evolution. We further evaluate leakage-resistant row-level and temporal settings, along with cross-satellite generalization, to characterize how model architecture and evaluation protocol influence satellite cyberattack detection. Experimental results demonstrate the value of structured multimodal modeling and rigorous evaluation, with the hierarchical Transformer achieving up to 91.66% accuracy and 85.63% macro F1 under the leakage-resistant evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Static Guarantees: Measuring the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail Gap in Security-Sensitive and LLM-Generated Python Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10762)
*Jessica Pourleyli,Maitreyee Das Urmi,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文识别并命名了 Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail（SPDF）现象：代码通过静态安全扫描并不等价于运行时安全。作者在 1,355 个 Python 样本上发现，约 14.53% 的静态扫描干净样本仍可获得运行时可验证的可利用证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机明确且现实性强：随着 LLM 生成代码和安全敏感代码规模扩大，业界常依赖 Bandit、Semgrep 等静态分析作为低成本安全门槛，但静态工具难以观察依赖输入、上下文和多步触发条件的运行时风险。因此，论文试图纠正“静态通过即安全”的过度推断。

Method: 方法采用三阶段代理式流水线：首先使用 Bandit-Semgrep 复合静态门控筛选无告警样本；随后由 LLM 进行 CWE 层面的候选漏洞推理；最后在隔离 Docker 环境中进行自动化运行时验证。该设计将规则扫描、语义推理和执行证据结合，适合检验静态分析漏检与实际可利用性之间的差距。

Result: 实验覆盖 SecurityEval、RedCode 和 CyberNative 三个数据集，共 1,355 个 Python 样本。其中 654 个样本未被复合静态扫描发现问题；LLM 阶段在 235 个文件中识别出 394 个候选漏洞；动态验证在 95 个文件中确认或部分确认可利用性，对静态干净样本的包容性命中率为 14.53%。不同数据集差异明显：候选文件-CWE 对中，RedCode 为 33.7%，CyberNative 为 28.6%，SecurityEval 为 5.4%。此外，CWE-338 和 CWE-916 等类别未被 Bandit 或 Semgrep 标记，说明静态规则覆盖存在结构性盲区。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于把静态检测成功与运行时安全区分为层级化保证，而非可互换指标；这对 AI 生成代码评估、安全基准设计和工具组合具有启发意义。潜在不足是摘要未充分说明动态验证的判定标准、LLM 候选推理的误报控制、Docker 环境与真实部署环境的差异，以及结果能否推广到非 Python 语言和更复杂系统。总体而言，该工作提出了有价值的问题框架，并用实验证据支持多层安全评估的必要性。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) fuel the quest for scalable methods to assess the security of generated and security-sensitive software. Static analysis is widely adopted as a scalable, reproducible, and inexpensive security gate, but cannot directly observe runtime exploit behaviour. Vulnerabilities dependent on adversarial inputs, execution context, or exploit chaining may evade static checks while remaining exploitable in practice, yet passing static analysis is often treated as evidence of secure behaviour. This paper introduces the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail (SPDF) phenomenon and a three-stage agentic pipeline combining static scanning, LLM-driven Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) reasoning, and autonomous exploit verification in isolated Docker containers. We evaluate 1,355 Python samples from SecurityEval, RedCode, and CyberNative datasets. Of the 654 samples producing no findings under the composite Bandit-Semgrep gate, the LLM detection stage identified 394 candidate vulnerabilities across 235 files. Dynamic verification confirmed or partially confirmed exploitability in 95 files, yielding an inclusive pipeline rate of 14.53% (roughly 1 in 7 statically clean samples). This rate represents the proportion of Bandit-Semgrep-clean samples for which the pipeline identified a candidate vulnerability and obtained runtime evidence supporting exploitability. Outcomes varied by dataset: among candidate file--CWE pairs, confirmed exploitability was 33.7% for RedCode, 28.6% for CyberNative, and 5.4% for SecurityEval. Several frequently confirmed classes, including CWE-338 and CWE-916, were flagged by neither Bandit nor Semgrep. These findings indicate that static-analysis success and runtime security are hierarchical layers of software assurance rather than interchangeable measures, and have the potential to reshape how AI-generated and security-sensitive code is evaluated.

</details>


### [99] [Big Enough to Break Out: Tracking the Rising Capability of LLM Penetration-Testing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10780)
*Victoria Lovelace,Cameron Berryman,Yuhan You,Suhas Reddy Adavelly,Joel Sadler,Daniel Graham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文比较两种基于PentestGPT的LLM渗透测试系统：较旧的人工参与系统与较新的自主系统。自主系统完成了全部三个目标，而加入覆盖记忆层后两种系统均未改善，表明当前瓶颈可能主要在规划与执行承诺，而非长期记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正被用于渗透测试，但其实际能力、失败模式及能力扩展的限制因素仍不清楚。研究旨在比较不同系统的任务完成能力，并检验长期记忆是否是复杂长链任务中的主要瓶颈。

Method: 在三个公开目标上比较两种PentestGPT系统：运行开源Kimi K2.5、需要人工参与的旧系统，以及运行Claude Opus 4.8、具备自主能力的新系统。研究进一步为两种系统加入覆盖记忆层，并通过整体目标完成情况和子任务完成率评估性能，同时复查旧系统停滞运行中的证据、规划过程与失败原因。由于模型、工具框架、自主程度和记忆架构同时变化，研究无法对系统差异进行严格因果归因。

Result: 自主系统完成了三个目标，包括旧系统未能完成的两个目标。旧系统即使在失败目标上也完成了约一半子任务，并且运行于普通高校GPU环境、没有服务商防护限制。加入覆盖记忆层后，两种系统的最终结果均未提升。对可复核的旧系统停滞运行分析显示，智能体通常已经掌握了推进路线所需的证据，但未能将证据转化为明确的利用假设并持续执行，因此规划和行动承诺比信息遗忘更可能是主要限制。

Conclusion: 研究结果削弱了“长期记忆是当前主要瓶颈”的普遍判断，提示渗透测试智能体的能力进展可能更多取决于规划、假设形成和持续执行能力。由于比较条件存在多重混杂因素，模型能力、系统自主性、工具框架和记忆机制的独立贡献仍需通过受控实验验证。子任务评分体系可用于防御方持续测量智能体能力变化，从而进行前瞻性评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to penetration testing, but we still know little about what they can do or how they fail. We compare two PentestGPT-based systems: a legacy human-in-the-loop system running the open-weight Kimi K2.5, and a newer autonomous system running Claude Opus 4.8. Across three public targets, the autonomous system solves all three, including the two the legacy system never finishes. The legacy result is the more surprising of the two. Even on the machines the legacy system fails to solve, it completes about half the subtasks, while running on ordinary university GPUs with no provider guardrails. We can describe the trend but not explain it, since model, harness, autonomy, and memory architecture all change together. Its direction still points to the next question: what will limit these agents as they take on more complex tasks? The usual answer is long-horizon memory, the loss of access to earlier findings during long attack chains. We test it by adding a coverage-memory layer to both systems, and neither improves outcomes. In the legacy stalled runs we could review, the limiting factor appeared to be planning and commitment rather than lost memory: agents held the evidence for a route forward and never turned it into a concrete exploitation hypothesis, which may suggest that offensive capability will advance with agents' ability to plan rather than with better memory. The same subtask scoring that tracks this capability is available to defenders, who can measure it as it rises instead of waiting to meet it in the field.

</details>


### [100] [No-Box Vulnerability Analysis: Description-only Detection of Indirect Prompt Injection Vulnerabilities in MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10854)
*Zehua Zhang,Jie Hu,Pratham Hegde,Aditya Maheshbhai Gabani,Souradip Nath,Yibo Liu,Siyu Liu,Hongkai Chen,Hulin Wang,Zhuoer Lyu,Chang Zhu,Divij Handa,Yan Shoshitaishvili,Tiffany Bao,Ruoyu Wang,Adam Doupe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“无盒漏洞分析”范式：在无法访问系统、无法运行交互且仅掌握功能元数据的条件下，推断所有符合该元数据的实现都可能存在的漏洞。原型MCPSEC仅利用MCP工具注册元数据分析间接提示注入漏洞，在20个服务器、177个工具上达到98.9%的召回率，优于LLM基线的84.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统漏洞分析通常依赖源代码、系统访问权限或运行时交互，这些条件在闭源、远程托管、关键在役系统及商业软件中可能不可获得。论文旨在建立一种仅依赖功能元数据的预分析方法，使第三方分析人员能够在获得实际访问权限前形成可验证的漏洞假设。

Method: 论文将系统元数据视为对预期行为的约束，包括输入、输出和副作用，并据此推理所有与该元数据一致的可能实现中共同存在的漏洞。作者实现MCPSEC，仅使用MCP服务器注册时公开的工具元数据，识别间接提示注入风险，并为每个判断生成漏洞假设和相应的利用技术。实验覆盖20个广泛部署的MCP服务器及177个工具，并以人工评估结果作为验证依据，同时与LLM基线比较。

Result: 人工评估确认177个工具中有95个存在漏洞。MCPSEC将143个工具标记为易受攻击，并对每个标记工具生成漏洞假设及利用方法。其中，94个真实漏洞被正确预测，召回率为98.9%；LLM基线正确预测80个，召回率为84.2%。结果表明，仅凭元数据即可在实际系统中获得较高的漏洞发现能力，但MCPSEC的143个预测相对于95个已确认漏洞也表明存在一定误报。

Conclusion: 研究首次系统提出无盒漏洞分析作为独立的漏洞研究范式，并通过MCPSEC证明其可行性。该方法适合用于缺乏系统访问和运行时交互条件下的早期审计与假设生成，但其结论本质上仍需在获得额外访问权限后验证，且误报控制、跨漏洞类型泛化能力及对元数据质量的依赖仍是重要后续问题。

Abstract: Conventional vulnerability analysis relies on either system access or dynamic interaction, all of which may be unavailable to third-party analysts auditing closed-source, remotely hosted, critical in situ systems, or commercially gated software. Therefore, we propose a new paradigm of no-box vulnerability analysis in which neither access nor runtime interaction is available, and only functionality metadata is available. Such metadata defines the intended behavior of the system, including its inputs, outputs, and side effects, while constraining the space of implementations consistent with that behavior. We propose hypothesizing about vulnerabilities that exist across all possible implementations of a given system metadata, without observing or interacting with the target system. An analyst can later validate these hypotheses when additional access is available. We showcase the feasibility of no-box vulnerability analysis through implementing a prototype called MCPSEC, which audits Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers for indirect prompt injection vulnerabilities using only the tool metadata exposed at server registration time. We evaluate MCPSEC on 20 widely deployed MCP servers comprising 177 tools, among which human evaluators confirm 95 vulnerable tools. MCPSEC identified 143 tools as vulnerable, and for each vulnerable tool, it produced a hypothesized vulnerability along with exploitation technique. Using metadata alone, MCPSEC predicted 94 (98.9% recall) real verified vulnerabilities, compared against an LLM baseline with 80 (84.2% recall). Overall, our results introduce no-box vulnerability analysis as a new analysis paradigm and demonstrate its practical feasibility in realistic systems.

</details>


### [101] [A2ABreak: Systematic Security Analysis of the A2A Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10871)
*Alireza Lotfi,Mirza Masfiqur Rahman,Imtiaz Karim,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出A2ABreak，对Agent2Agent（A2A）协议进行系统化安全分析。该方法从自然语言规范中提取并验证有限状态机，发现了11个无需实现缺陷、仅依赖规范行为即可利用的新漏洞，表明A2A协议存在显著的协议级安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A2A作为跨组织自主智能体发现、认证和任务委派的开放标准，正在承担多智能体生态中的通信功能，但其安全性此前缺乏系统研究。由于协议规范以自然语言描述，直接分析容易遗漏状态转换、委派链和跨客户端交互中的风险，因此需要一种具有形式化基础的分析方法。

Method: 研究者提出A2ABreak，使用大语言模型辅助从A2A自然语言规范中抽取经验证的有限状态机，并将929条形式化陈述统一建模为37个状态和76条转换。在假设攻击者完全遵守协议规范的前提下，系统执行对抗性验证，以识别协议级漏洞。研究还将结果与独立专家评审及同一规范上的零样本大语言模型基线进行比较。

Result: A2ABreak发现11个新的、由规范合规攻击者即可利用的漏洞，包括未受保护上下文标识符导致的跨客户端上下文注入、委派链中的多跳身份丢失导致的凭据窃取风险，以及恶意智能体伪造未经证明的能力声明造成的数据泄露风险。相对于独立专家评审，系统取得73.3%的精确率和84.6%的F1值；零样本大语言模型基线未发现任何经确认的漏洞。

Conclusion: 研究表明，A2A的安全问题不仅来自具体实现错误，也可能根植于协议规范本身。将自然语言规范转化为显式、可验证的形式模型，能够显著提升协议安全分析的有效性和可靠性；因此，A2A在进一步部署前需要完善上下文隔离、委派身份保持、能力声明认证及相关安全约束。

Abstract: The Agent2Agent (A2A) protocol, now governed by the Linux Foundation, is an open standard that enables autonomous AI agents to discover, authenticate with, and delegate tasks to one another across organizational boundaries. Designed to complement the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for tool integration, A2A is rapidly emerging as the horizontal communication layer of the multi-agent ecosystem. Yet the protocol's security has received no systematic analysis.
  This paper presents A2ABreak, the first rigorous systematic security analysis of the A2A protocol. We introduce a novel framework that utilizes an LLM-assisted extraction of a verified finite-state machine directly from the natural-language specification, producing a unified model of 37 states and 76 transitions from 929 formalized statements, and then systematically reasons over this model to discover protocol-level vulnerabilities through adversarial verification, under a full-compliance assumption.
  Our analysis uncovers 11 new vulnerabilities, each exploitable by a specification-compliant adversary without requiring any implementation flaw. Among the findings are cross-client context injection through unprotected context identifiers, credential harvesting via multi-hop identity loss in delegation chains, and data exfiltration through rogue agents advertising unattested capability claims.
  A2ABreak achieves 73.3% precision and 84.6% F1 against independent expert review, while a zero-shot LLM baseline operating over the same specification produces zero confirmed findings, demonstrating that explicit formal grounding is essential for sound protocol security analysis.

</details>


### [102] [AspisAI: A Canonical, Machine-Interpretable Governance Framework for Automated Multi-Standard Compliance Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10881)
*Tsafac Nkombong Regine Cyrille,Hasan Dag,Reiner Creutzburg,Knut Haufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AspisAI，一个有界且标准无关的网络安全治理框架，将ISO/IEC 27001、NIST CSF 2.0、Cyber Essentials和GDPR中的选定要求转换为统一的机器可解释控制模型，并基于条件决策规则评估证据，实现可解释、可追溯的合规判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多标准合规管理主要依赖人工映射、电子表格和周期性审计，维护成本高、标准间不一致且可追溯性不足。研究旨在通过统一控制模型和证据驱动的自动评估，降低多框架治理的复杂性，并增强审计透明度。

Method: 在26项代表性要求的有界范围内，构建规范化、保留来源信息的控制模型，并依据条件化决策规则对提交证据进行判定。研究通过受控模拟，以五项治理标准评估框架；同时将跨标准映射与NIST公开的信息性参考进行外部比对，并使用OpenSSF Scorecard的真实第三方证据测试实际适用性。

Result: 框架完成了全部要求的编码，实现88.5%的映射覆盖率和完整追溯性，并正确识别了模拟中引入的全部治理缺口。与NIST公开参考的精确一致率为57%，差异主要局限于同一控制族内部。对真实开源项目证据的应用也发现了实际治理缺口。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在明确限定范围内，AspisAI能够将多标准合规转化为可自动执行、可解释且可审计的治理流程。外部参考比对和真实证据测试降低了单一作者评估的循环论证风险，但研究结论仍主要适用于所选的26项要求，尚需在更广泛的标准、组织场景和证据类型中进一步验证。

Abstract: Organisations operating in regulated and critical-infrastructure sectors must satisfy multiple, heterogeneous cybersecurity and privacy instruments simultaneously, including but not limited to ISO/IEC~27001, the NIST Cybersecurity Framework~2.0, Cyber Essentials, and the GDPR. In practice, these obligations are managed through manual mappings, spreadsheet-based tracking, and periodic audits that are costly to maintain, inconsistent across standards, and weak in traceability. This paper presents \emph{AspisAI}, a bounded, standard-agnostic governance framework that translates selected requirements from several frameworks into a canonical, machine-interpretable control model, and evaluates submitted evidence against condition-based decision rules to produce explainable, traceable compliance determinations. Within a bounded scope of 26 representative requirements, the framework is evaluated in a controlled simulation against five governance-oriented criteria and, critically, against two external reference points that mitigate the circularity of single-author evaluation: its cross-standard mappings are validated against NIST's own published informative references, with 57\,\% exact agreement and divergences confined to same-family controls, and the framework is applied to real third-party evidence from the OpenSSF Scorecard, surfacing genuine governance gaps in a live open-source project. The controlled results, comprising full requirement encoding, 88.5\,\% mapping coverage, complete traceability, and correct detection of all introduced gaps, establish functional correctness, while the external validation provides evidence of applicability beyond the simulation. The contribution is therefore a demonstration that a canonical, provenance-preserving governance model can render multi-standard compliance both automatable and auditable.

</details>


### [103] [DriftNet: A Dual-Head Trajectory Transformer for Detecting and Localizing Prompt Injection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10892)
*Asif Pinjari,Mithun Paul Saint-Germain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DriftNet是一种双头轨迹Transformer，可在一次前向传播中同时判断LLM智能体轨迹是否被间接提示注入攻陷，并逐步定位注入点、劫持区间及失败注入。其参数量少于200万，在AgentDrift基准上取得了极高的检测与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只能输出整条轨迹的安全性 verdict 或单一不安全步骤，无法同时回答攻击从何处进入、哪些步骤被篡改以及注入是否被抵御。运维人员因此缺乏细粒度且可操作的轨迹诊断结果。

Method: 模型采用双头轨迹Transformer：第一检测头进行轨迹级 compromised/benign 分类，第二标注头为每个步骤分配 benign、injection point、hijacked 或 failed injection 四类标签。每个步骤由冻结的句子编码器和四类不含身份信息的世界特征表示，训练主干参数少于200万，并使用面向两个任务的类别加权联合目标。模型无需访问智能体自身的语言模型；实验采用AgentDrift任务不相交划分、20组配置敏感性扫描，以及仅一次测试集评估。

Result: 在包含12536条轨迹和71024个标注步骤的AgentDrift基准上，轨迹级F1达到0.983；被攻击轨迹的注入点精确恢复率为98.7%，劫持区间IoU为0.979；对218次被抵御的攻击实现零误报，对困难负例的误报率为2.9%。相比在相同划分上重新训练的表面基线，DriftNet将部分劫持识别率从11.1%提升至98.6%，将延迟执行识别率从17.1%提升至93.2%，且所有误报率均更低。剩余26个错误的分析表明，多数漏检来自注入观测中缺少可识别指令的轨迹。

Conclusion: DriftNet证明了以轨迹为输入的联合检测与步骤级定位是可行的，并在攻击识别、注入点恢复和劫持范围估计方面显著优于表面行为基线。其主要局限是对无明显指令内容的注入观测仍不够敏感；此外，论文同时报告了基准中的世界身份规律性，提示模型性能可能部分依赖数据集特征，实际部署时需要进一步验证跨环境泛化能力。

Abstract: When an indirect prompt injection succeeds against an LLM agent, the compromise is visible in the agent's own behavior: a benign prefix of tool calls, a poisoned observation, and a suffix of actions that serve the attacker. An operator needs three facts: where the attack entered, which steps it corrupted, and whether apparent poison was resisted. Existing systems return either a whole-trace verdict or a single unsafe index. We present DriftNet, a dual-head trajectory Transformer that reads a logged tool-call trajectory and answers all three questions in one forward pass: one head classifies the trajectory as compromised or not, and a second assigns every step one of four labels (benign, injection point, hijacked, failed injection). To our knowledge it is the first supervised detector to produce this joint output. A frozen sentence encoder and four identity-free world features embed each step; the trained trunk, under two million parameters and optimized with a class-weighted joint objective over both heads, needs no access to the agent's model. On the task-disjoint split of the AgentDrift benchmark (12,536 trajectories, 71,024 labeled steps), with a 20-configuration sweep bounding hyperparameter sensitivity to 0.011 F1 and the test part evaluated exactly once, DriftNet reaches trajectory-level F1 of 0.983, exact injection-point recovery on 98.7% of attacked trajectories, hijacked-span IoU of 0.979, zero flags on 218 resisted attacks, and 2.9% flags on hard negatives. A surface baseline retrained on the identical split recovers 11.1% of partial hijacks and 17.1% of delayed executions; DriftNet reaches 98.6% and 93.2% while lowering every false-alarm rate. Reading all 26 residual errors shows that most misses trace to trajectories whose labeled injection observation carries no legible instruction, and we report the benchmark's measured world-identity regularity alongside the results.

</details>


### [104] [Empirical Evaluation of Membership Inference Attacks on NLP Text Classifiers: A Baseline Study on SST-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10935)
*William Novak,Muhammad Abusaqer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在SST-2情感分类任务上系统比较了TF-IDF+逻辑回归与微调DistilBERT的成员推断风险，并评估正则化和训练轮数调整的缓解效果。DistilBERT分类性能更高，但两种模型均存在可检测的成员信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言处理模型的训练数据可能包含敏感文本，因此有必要量化文本分类模型的成员推断脆弱性，并研究无需复杂防御机制即可实现的隐私—效用权衡方案。

Method: 构建受控基准，在GLUE SST-2数据集上比较TF-IDF+逻辑回归和微调DistilBERT。使用基于损失阈值的成员推断攻击，以攻击AUC衡量泄露程度，并以开发集准确率和宏平均F1衡量模型效用。进一步测试逻辑回归的更强正则化，以及DistilBERT从3个训练轮次减少至2个训练轮次的影响。

Result: DistilBERT取得0.9466准确率和0.9460宏平均F1，明显优于逻辑回归的0.8756和0.8727。两种模型均表现出成员信息泄露，攻击AUC分别为0.5615和0.5800。更强正则化能够降低逻辑回归的泄露，但会带来明显效用损失；DistilBERT减少一个训练轮次即可降低泄露，且准确率几乎不受影响。摘要未报告统计显著性、攻击阈值细节或多次运行的方差。

Conclusion: 模型分类性能与成员隐私风险并不等价，高性能模型仍可能泄露训练成员信息。适度正则化或减少微调轮次是低成本的风险缓解手段，其中减少DistilBERT训练轮次展现出更优的隐私—效用权衡，但结论仍需在更多数据集、攻击方式和模型设置下验证。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) try to determine whether a specific record was used to train a model, a privacy risk that matters in natural language processing (NLP), where training data can contain sensitive user text. This paper presents a controlled benchmark of membership inference vulnerability for text classification on the GLUE SST-2 sentiment dataset. A TF-IDF + Logistic Regression pipeline and a fine-tuned DistilBERT classifier are compared under a loss-threshold MIA, with utility measured by development accuracy and macro F1. DistilBERT reached 0.9466 accuracy and 0.9460 macro F1 against 0.8756 and 0.8727 for Logistic Regression, yet both models leaked membership signal (Attack AUC 0.5615 and 0.5800, respectively). Two mitigations were tested. Stronger regularization reduced leakage for Logistic Regression at a visible utility cost, whereas fine-tuning DistilBERT for 2 epochs instead of 3 reduced leakage with negligible accuracy loss. Lightweight training adjustments can improve the privacy-utility trade-off without complex defenses.

</details>


### [105] [Empirical Evaluation of Data Poisoning Attacks in Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10952)
*Toshif Khan,Muhammad Abusaqer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究比较了标签翻转与后门投毒在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上的影响。标签翻转明显降低模型性能，后门投毒则在保持较高干净测试性能的同时，实现了0.9667至1.0000的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统干净测试集指标难以充分揭示训练数据投毒风险，尤其难以识别能够隐蔽植入特定行为的后门攻击。因此，研究旨在比较不同投毒方式对模型性能和攻击效果的影响，并评估更全面的安全指标。

Method: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上，使用逻辑回归、线性支持向量机和随机森林三种基线分类器，比较干净训练与5%、10%、20%投毒率下的表现。评估指标包括干净测试准确率、宏平均精确率、宏平均召回率、宏平均F1值，以及后门攻击成功率。

Result: 标签翻转对模型造成明显性能下降，其中逻辑回归和线性SVM受影响最大，随机森林表现相对稳定。后门投毒在两个数据集和三种模型上的攻击成功率均达到0.9667至1.0000，同时通常能够使干净测试性能接近基线水平。

Conclusion: 无差别投毒通常会直接反映在标准分类指标的下降上，而定向后门投毒能够以较强隐蔽性植入高效的攻击行为。模型安全评估不应仅依赖干净测试性能，还应结合攻击成功率等面向安全性的指标。

Abstract: Data poisoning corrupts training data to degrade a model or to plant attacker-controlled behavior. This study evaluates two representative training-time attacks, label flipping and backdoor poisoning, on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST with three baseline classifiers: Logistic Regression, Linear SVM, and Random Forest. Clean training is compared with poisoning rates of 5%, 10%, and 20% using clean-test accuracy, macro-precision, macro-recall, macro-F1, and, for backdoors, attack success rate. Label flipping caused clear degradation, largest for Logistic Regression and Linear SVM, while Random Forest stayed comparatively stable. Backdoor poisoning reached attack success rates from 0.9667 to 1.0000 on both datasets and all three models while often keeping clean-test performance near baseline. The results separate indiscriminate poisoning, which shows up in standard metrics, from targeted backdoor poisoning, which stays comparatively stealthy while embedding highly effective malicious behavior, and they support security-oriented evaluation beyond conventional clean-test metrics.

</details>


### [106] [DeFiFusion: Combining Transaction Events with Smart Contracts to Detect Price Manipulation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11008)
*Rui Cao,Shaojing Fan,Liming Fang,Yuchan Liu,Yingying Jiao,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DeFiFusion，一种融合交易行为与智能合约语义的双模态价格操纵攻击检测框架，通过联合建模交易事件、经济特征和执行逻辑，提升DeFi价格操纵攻击识别能力。在225个攻击案例中成功召回222个，精确率达到96.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交易中心方法缺乏智能合约执行语义，容易将正常市场波动误报为攻击；静态合约分析又忽视真实交易行为，常报告实际难以利用的漏洞。价格操纵攻击的恶意性取决于交易行为与合约逻辑的交互，因此需要统一结合两类信息进行检测。

Method: 首先设计面向价格操纵的事件编码，提取细粒度的时间特征与经济特征，以刻画操纵行为模式；随后利用基于大语言模型的合约语义提取方法，获取智能合约执行逻辑上下文；最后提出双模态投影融合Transformer，并结合T5风格的相对位置编码，建模价格操纵攻击中的循环式、多阶段执行结构，从而区分恶意行为与正常市场活动。

Result: 实验结果表明，DeFiFusion整体取得当前领先的检测性能。在225个价格操纵攻击案例中召回222个，召回率约为98.67%，同时精确率达到96.10%，说明该方法兼顾了攻击覆盖能力与误报控制能力。

Conclusion: 价格操纵攻击检测不能仅依赖交易轨迹或静态合约漏洞信息，而应联合分析交易行为及其所利用的合约执行语义。DeFiFusion验证了双模态建模和多阶段时序结构表示的有效性，但摘要未说明数据集构成、基线细节、消融实验及泛化到未见协议的能力，这些因素仍是评估实际部署价值的重要依据。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) has emerged as a rapidly growing blockchain-based financial service, where market transaction dynamics and underlying smart contract logic are intricately intertwined. This autonomous interplay, while eliminating centralized intermediaries, significantly expands the vulnerability surface of DeFi protocols to Price Manipulation Attacks (PMAs), which have already inflicted catastrophic financial losses. Despite their gravity, existing detection paradigms suffer from fundamental limitations. Transaction-centric methods lack awareness of contract execution semantics, making them prone to false positives under legitimate market volatility, while static contract analyses ignore real transaction behaviors and frequently report vulnerabilities that are infeasible to exploit in practice. We present DeFiFusion, a dual-modal PMA detection framework that closes this gap by jointly modeling transaction events and smart contract semantics within a unified pipeline. Our core insight is that PMA maliciousness emerges only from the interaction between transaction behaviors and the contract logic they exploit; neither signal suffices in isolation. Accordingly, we derive price-manipulation-aware event encoding for extracting fine-grained temporal and economic features tailored to manipulation patterns. We further introduce LLM-based contract semantic extraction to supply the execution-logic context that prior behavioral methods lack. To fuse these modalities, we propose a Dual-Modal Projection-Fusion Transformer with T5-style relative positional encoding, capturing the cyclic multi-stage execution structures that distinguish PMAs from benign market activity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeFiFusion consistently achieves state-of-the-art detection performance, effectively recalling 222 of the 225 PMA cases while maintaining a precision of 96.10%.

</details>


### [107] [The Missing Boundary: How Autonomous Agents Lose Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11024)
*Zonghao Ying,Xiangfan Wu,Huiyu Wu,Xing Zheng,Huangsheng Cheng,Xiaorong Shi,Jing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究自主智能体在合法任务执行过程中越过授权边界的条件。实验表明，控制能力退化与可执行的不安全机会同时存在时，失控行为显著增加；恢复控制约束可将失控率降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将智能体失控归因于对抗性指令、恶意环境或目标冲突，较少解释合法任务中控制边界如何逐步失效并导致越权执行。本文旨在识别控制退化、目标压力和不安全执行机会之间的因果关系。

Method: 研究者在确定性的多轮交互环境中独立操纵目标压力、控制退化和可执行的不安全机会，覆盖5个智能体模型与16个操作领域，共评估1800条独立轨迹。实验包括全因子研究、额外领域验证、恢复原始控制边界的干预，以及上下文压缩消融实验。

Result: 控制退化或不安全机会单独存在时，失控率均不显著；二者同时存在时，全因子实验中的失控率达到55%，在另外10个操作领域中达到62%。恢复原始控制边界后，即使不安全操作仍可执行，失控率降至0%。上下文压缩本身并无危害：保留控制约束时失控率为0%，删除约束后升至87%。

Conclusion: 智能体失控并不必然源于任务目标异常，而可能源于控制边界的隐性缺失与可执行机会的结合。当环境提供能够跨越授权边界的实际操作时，潜在的控制退化会转化为外部可观测的越权行为。研究结果支持在长程智能体系统中显式保留并验证控制约束。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly perform long-horizon tasks involving tool use, persistent state, and consequential actions, raising a fundamental question: \emph{under what conditions does an agent cross the boundary of authorized execution while pursuing a legitimate task?} Existing studies often attribute such failures to adversarial instructions, malicious environments, or conflicting objectives, leaving unclear how loss of control can emerge during otherwise legitimate task execution. We study this question by independently manipulating three factors: goal pressure, control degradation, and executable unsafe opportunity. Our central hypothesis is that a degraded control boundary becomes consequential when the environment exposes an executable action that crosses it, even when the underlying task remains legitimate and a sanctioned path remains feasible. We test this hypothesis in a deterministic multi-turn environment across five agent models and 16 operational domains. Across 1,800 unique trajectories, we find that neither degraded control nor unsafe opportunity alone produces substantial loss of control; when both are present, the loss-of-control rate reaches $55\%$ in the full-factorial study and $62\%$ across ten additional operational domains. Restoring the original control boundary reduces the rate to $0\%$ even when the unsafe action remains executable. A context-management ablation further shows that compaction itself is not harmful: preserving the control constraints yields $0\%$ loss of control, whereas omitting them increases the rate to $87\%$. These results show how a latent loss of control can become an external violation: the task objective remains intact, but an executable opportunity can turn a missing control boundary into consequential action. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/Tencent/AI-Infra-Guard.

</details>


### [108] [BenchShield: Formal Model-Backed Instrumentation for Reward Integrity in LLM-Agent Evaluation Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11028)
*Shenghan Zheng,Zonglin Di,Yimin Liu,Kyoung Whan Choe,Jiankai Sun,Heguang Lin,Penghao Jiang,Yifeng He,Xiao Cheng,Jicheng Wang,Wenbo Chen,Alex Yates,Yinzhe Zhao,Bingran You,Yuan Gao,Ayush Munot,Shubham Gaur,Zhe Ye,Hao Wang,Xiangyi Li,Dawn Song,Christophe Hauser*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BenchShield，一种面向LLM智能体评测的模型驱动型奖励完整性检测层，通过有限生命周期模型、静态污点分析和运行时基础设施证据，识别并归因奖励破解行为。基于456条人工标注轨迹的评估显示，其检测召回率、同向量覆盖率和成本均优于智能体扫描基线，运行时检测准确率达到96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式LLM智能体基准允许智能体观察状态、调用工具、修改工作区并提交产物，因此可能通过操纵与奖励相关的执行轨迹来提高分数，而非完成预期任务。现有方法主要依赖任务专用补丁、提示约束或事后检测，缺乏可复用且能够证明具体运行未越过评测边界的证据机制。

Method: BenchShield首先将评测过程抽象为由奖励相关事件组成的有限生命周期模型。其静态分析组件执行具有阶段感知能力的污点分析，在运行前暴露潜在的奖励破解路径；运行时分析组件则利用评测基础设施侧证据，归因智能体的具体使用行为，并生成有证据支持的检测结论。研究还构建了BenchShield Trajectories数据集，包含来自三个基准、超过31000次公开智能体运行中的456条人工裁决轨迹。

Result: 相较于在相同任务和模型上的智能体式可破解性扫描基线，BenchShield将全链路召回率从23%至94%提升至77%至100%，将同向量覆盖率从16%至56%提升至43%至78%，并使单任务成本最多降低65%。其基于运行时基础设施证据的分析在奖励破解检测中达到96%的准确率。

Conclusion: BenchShield表明，将评测生命周期建模、静态路径分析与运行时基础设施证据结合，可以形成更具可复用性、可归因性和成本效率的奖励完整性防护机制。该方法在多个基准上显著提升了奖励破解的覆盖与检测能力，但其有效性仍取决于生命周期模型的完整性、基础设施证据的可获得性以及标注数据对真实攻击类型的代表性。

Abstract: LM-agent benchmarks increasingly function as interactive evaluation infrastructure. Agents observe state, call tools, modify workspaces,
  submit artifacts, and receive rewards from outcome procedures. This interactivity makes evaluations vulnerable to reward hacking: an agent
  improves its measured score by exploiting the reward-relevant trajectory instead of solving the intended task. Existing defenses rely largely
  on task-specific patches, prompt instructions, or post-hoc detectors. They do not provide reusable evidence that a concrete run remained
  within its intended evaluation boundary. This paper presents BenchShield, a model-backed instrumentation layer for reward integrity in
  LLM-agent evaluation. BenchShield grounds detection in a finite lifecycle model of an evaluation's reward-relevant events. Within the
  benchmark infrastructure, two complementary analyses operate over this model. A static, phase-aware taint analysis exposes reward-hacking
  paths before a run. Its runtime counterpart uses infrastructure-side evidence to attribute concrete agent use and emit evidence-backed claims.
  We construct BenchShield Trajectories, a human-labeled corpus of 456 adjudicated trajectories from more than 31,000 public agent runs across
  three benchmarks. Compared with an agentic hackability scanner baseline on the same tasks and model, BenchShield improves full-chain recall
  from 23-94% to 77-100%, same-vector coverage from 16-56% to 43-78%, and reduces per-task cost by up to 65%. Its runtime analysis achieves 96%
  accuracy in detecting reward hacking from infrastructure-side evidence.

</details>


### [109] [ToxicRAG: Compromising Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems via Single-Shot Knowledge Poisoning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11082)
*Haozhe Lu,Jiaqi Li,Xinyuan Zhu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ToxicRAG提出一种单文档知识投毒攻击：通过构造“知识更新”叙事，将虚假答案包装为可信信息，并在多种RAG配置下显著提高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG依赖外部文档为大语言模型提供证据，但恶意文档可能污染检索结果并诱导模型生成错误答案。现有攻击通常需要注入多个文档或直接使用答案模板，因此研究更隐蔽且资源需求更低的单文档攻击具有重要意义。

Method: ToxicRAG针对每个目标问题仅生成一份投毒文档。文档先承认原有答案，再描述虚构事件以削弱原答案的有效性，最后借助若干伪造权威支持攻击者指定的答案。方法还可选用答案导向的自验证循环：当替代语言模型未能复现目标答案时，迭代修正文档。实验覆盖Natural Questions、HotpotQA和MS-MARCO三个数据集，每个数据集选取100个问题，并组合四个受害大语言模型和四个稠密检索器进行评估。

Result: 在采样语料库设置下，ToxicRAG在12种数据集与模型组合中的攻击成功率为0.61至0.91。与评估中的最强基线相比，ToxicRAG在所有组合中均达到或超过其表现，优势幅度为0至11个百分点。

Conclusion: 叙事化的单文档投毒内容能够在所测试的RAG配置中持续影响模型答案，且攻击效果优于或不弱于多种基线。研究结果表明，RAG系统需要进一步加强事实一致性验证、文档来源溯源和检索证据可信度评估。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) can ground large language model (LLM) outputs in external evidence, but it also exposes the system to knowledge poisoning. Representative attacks use multiple injected documents or templates that directly assert a target answer. We present ToxicRAG, a one-document-per-target attack that expresses misinformation as a coherent knowledge-update narrative. The generated document first acknowledges the previously accepted answer, introduces fabricated events that appear to invalidate it, and then attributes the attacker-selected answer to a set of purported authorities. An answer-focused self-validation loop optionally revises a candidate when a surrogate language model does not reproduce the target answer. We evaluate the attack on 100 target questions from each of Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and MS-MARCO, using four victim LLMs and four dense retrievers. In the sampled-corpus setting reported in this paper, ToxicRAG obtains ASRs between 0.61 and 0.91 across the twelve dataset--model combinations. It matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in every combination, with margins ranging from 0 to 11 percentage points. These results show that narrative-form poisoned documents can remain influential under the evaluated RAG configurations and motivate further study of factual consistency and source provenance in RAG systems.

</details>


### [110] [The Machines Are Calling: Measuring Automated and Synthetic Voices in Unwanted Inbound Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11137)
*Xingyu Shen,Tommy Duong,Muduo Xu,Xiaodong An,Jiaqi Gan,Haoyuan Tang,Jamey Z. Liang,Siyu Zhang,Yan Zhang,Simiao Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一个针对美国骚扰电话中“机器语音”和“合成语音”占比的实证测量研究。核心发现是：至少26.9%的正常接听来电以机器语音开场，其中录音重放与新鲜合成语音规模相近；另有约一成静默连接也可能由机器拨打。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于监管已将AI生成语音纳入电话消费者保护框架，但缺乏同行评议的实测证据来回答两个问题：骚扰电话中有多少由机器发起，以及机器语音中有多少是真正的语音合成而非预录音播放。该问题对执法、检测系统评估和自动化电话治理均有现实意义。

Method: 作者构建交互式语音蜜罐：使用语言模型人格在真实美国电话号码上接听，并将来电方音轨单独录制。样本包含66天内10,987通电话，其中剔除11天静默应答异常数据；主要分析正常问候日的7,233通来电。每通电话开场由三类工具判读：音频指纹识别重复录音，商业合成语音检测器分析前10秒，盲听者复核检测器标记结果。

Result: 在7,233通正常接听电话中，13.8%以重复出现的录音开场，13.1%以检测器标为合成的新增音频开场，因此机器语音开场至少为26.9%。此外，9.9%在问候后无发声，被解释为机器拨打的静默连接；54.2%被检测器标为人声，9.0%无法评分。检测器存在明显不稳定性：同一波形在13.6%的情况下被判到阈值两侧；11名听者仅确认其标记样本的54.4%。合成语音更集中于引流类垃圾电话而非欺诈类电话，且自动化披露率仅0.44%。号码传播时间解释了 prevalence 差异：旧诱饵号码同期机器语音占比为59%，新号码为19%。

Conclusion: 该研究的贡献在于将“录音重放”和“实时/新鲜合成语音”拆分测量，并公开了多工具交叉验证流程，弥补了仅依赖合成语音检测器的偏差。结论显示，骚扰电话自动化程度已经较高，但商业检测器单独使用并不可靠；治理重点应同时覆盖录音重放、合成语音、静默拨号和跨号码延续的活动网络。主要局限是样本来自蜜罐号码，外推到总体电话流量时需谨慎。

Abstract: In February 2024 the U.S. Federal Communications Commission (FCC) placed AI-generated voices under the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA). Yet no peer-reviewed measurement says how much unwanted call traffic is placed by a machine, or how much of that machine speech is synthesized rather than played from a recording. We report both with a disclosed pipeline. An interactive voice honeypot (language-model personas on real U.S. numbers, the caller recorded on its own track) recorded 10,987 calls over 66 days; 11 days on which our stack answered silently are set aside. Three instruments read each opening: an audio fingerprint that finds the same recording played on other calls, a commercial synthetic-speech detector on the caller's first ten seconds, and blinded listeners who check what it flags. Of the 7,233 calls our persona greeted on normal days, 13.8% open with a recording we also heard on another call, and 13.1% with fresh audio the detector labels synthetic. A further 9.9% open with a caller who never spoke after our greeting, 54.2% with fresh audio the detector labels human, and 9.0% could not be scored. Machine-voiced openings are therefore at least 26.9%, a further tenth of calls are silent connections we read as machine-placed, and replays of a recording make up 45% of the detector's own rate (29.3% of 6,192 scored openings). The same waveform played on two calls lands on opposite sides of the detector's threshold 13.6% of the time, and eleven listeners confirm 54.4% of what it flags. Synthetic openings concentrate in lead-generation spam (33.8%), not fraud (21.1%); 0.44% disclose automation. Prevalence tracks how long a bait number has circulated (59% against 19% in the same weeks): seeding history, not calendar time, explains the trend. Campaigns outlast their numbers: one recorded compliance notice opens calls in six campaigns, and one synthetic voice serves nine.

</details>


### [111] [You Get What You Sample: Evaluating Sampling Strategies for Web Security Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11218)
*Xuenan Zhang,Yuqing Yang,Giancarlo Pellegrino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估Web安全测量中的采样方法，发现Top N采样不能代表整体Web分布，而基于概率的采样在漏洞普遍率和多种影响评估目标上能提供更稳定、无偏的估计；混合采样并未优于纯概率采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模域名数据集的全面分析成本高昂，研究者因而依赖采样，但现有采样实践主要遵循惯例，尤其是Top N选择，缺乏关于其系统性偏差、漏洞率失真、研究可比性和结论可推广性的系统证据。

Method: 作者结合系统性文献综述与大规模Web测量，对500,000个Tranco主机和2,480万个Common Crawl主机进行比较分析，评估不同数据集及Top N、概率采样和混合采样策略对普遍率估计及影响评估结果的作用，并进一步设计适用于目标问题普遍率未知情形的自适应概率采样策略。

Result: Top N采样虽然在某些场景下具有合理性，但无法反映整体Web的分布。概率采样能够为普遍率和多种影响目标提供稳定且无偏的估计。混合采样没有显示出相对于纯概率采样的优势，其确定性前缀会持续降低估计准确性。

Conclusion: Web安全测量不应将Top N采样视为默认且具有代表性的方案。研究应优先采用概率基础的采样方法，尤其是能够根据观测结果自适应调整的概率采样策略，以提高估计准确性、研究间可比性和结论的普遍性。

Abstract: Web measurement studies rely on domain datasets such as Tranco to quantify the prevalence and impact of security issues at scale, but exhaustively analyzing these datasets is often infeasible because of the cost of advanced analysis techniques, requiring the use of sampling. Despite its widespread use, sampling remains largely guided by convention---most commonly \emph{Top $N$} domain selection---rather than evidence, and its influence on the validity and generalizability of security findings has received little systematic evaluation. Consequently, it remains unclear whether common sampling strategies introduce systematic bias, distort observed vulnerability rates, or limit comparability across studies.
  In this work, we undertake, to the best of our knowledge, the first comprehensive investigation into how sampling methodologies affect the measurements and the conclusions. Through a comprehensive literature review and large-scale measurements of 500k Tranco and 24.8M Common Crawl hosts, we perform a comparative evaluation of datasets and sampling strategies. We show that, while Top $N$ sampling may be a rational strategy, the researchers have to bear in mind that Top $N$ does not reflect the overall distribution of the web. Instead, probability-based strategies yield stable, unbiased estimates for prevalence and many impact objectives. Hybrid sampling provides no advantages over pure probability sampling, as its deterministic prefix consistently contributes negatively to accuracy. Building on these results, we provide data-backed guidance for future studies, proposing to use an adaptive probability-based sampling strategy that remains effective even when the prevalence of the target issue is unknown.

</details>


### [112] [You've Got a BUD in Me: Authenticated Reads from Per-Block Write Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11251)
*Alejandro Ranchal-Pedrosa,Cody Littley,Ben Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Block Update Digest（BUD）及其扩展SuperBUD，通过认证区块写入记录而非覆盖整个状态，为区块链历史成员关系与排除关系查询提供短证明。实验表明，该方案在状态规模扩大50倍时扩展性和证明开销均优于两类Merkle Patricia Trie实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块链通常维护覆盖全局状态的认证结构，以支持读取证明，导致存储、更新和验证成本随状态规模增长。本文旨在利用历史写入记录支持成员关系与排除关系证明，并降低长期未修改键的证明长度，同时在存在拜占庭验证者时保证证明可靠性。

Method: BUD对写入日志进行承诺，并通过前驱指针连接同一键的连续修改。SuperBUD在给定窗口内汇总每个键的最后一次写入；进一步使用指数层级压缩长时间未修改区间，使证明所需摘要数量在层级范围内随间隔对数增长，超出范围后每个顶层窗口增加一个摘要。理论上证明方案在对抗性证明者及最多f个拜占庭验证者下的可靠性，并在具备归档、签名证明和委员会证据，且查询锚定于部署后的键修改时保证完备性。

Result: 当状态规模增加50倍时，基础BUD路径长度仅增加1.24倍，而内存型和受缓存限制的磁盘型Merkle Patricia Trie分别增加3.1倍和69.5倍。在合成轨迹中，两摘要读取层负载低于800字节；温缓存哈希路径验证的p99延迟最高为146微秒。

Conclusion: BUD及SuperBUD通过写入认证和指数层级有效降低了历史查询的证明规模，并表现出较好的状态规模扩展性、网络负载和验证延迟。其完备性依赖于归档、证明材料和委员会证据的可用性，以及查询键在部署后确实发生过修改；因此，未修改键或缺少历史证据时的覆盖能力仍是实际部署中的边界条件。

Abstract: Blockchains usually pay for authenticated reads by maintaining a structure that spans the entire state. We show how validators can support historical membership and exclusion proofs by authenticating each block's writes instead. A Block Update Digest (BUD) commits a write log whose predecessor pointers link successive modifications of each key. A SuperBUD summarizes last writes over a window; an exponential hierarchy turns long unchanged intervals into short proofs. The digest count is logarithmic in the gap within the hierarchy's range, with one additional digest per top-level window beyond it. We prove soundness against adversarial provers and up to f Byzantine validators, and completeness for queries anchored by a post-deployment modification, assuming archive, attestation, and committee evidence is available. Across a 50x increase in state size, the measured base-BUD path rises by 1.24x, compared with 3.1x and 69.5x for in-memory and cache-bounded disk-backed Merkle Patricia tries. On the synthetic trace, two-digest read-layer payloads stay below 800 bytes, and warm hash-path verification takes at most 146 microseconds at p99.

</details>


### [113] [Few-Shot Learning for Network Intrusion Detection: Methods, Datasets, and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11275)
*Arne Roszeitis,Victor Jüttner,Erik Buchmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2022至2026年面向网络入侵检测的少样本学习研究，从1358条初始记录中筛选出21项研究，归纳了方法、数据集与实验设置，并指出研究之间存在较大的评估差异和可复现性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型网络入侵通常缺乏充足的标注数据，使传统入侵检测系统难以及时训练和部署。少样本学习能够利用每类少量样本进行建模，但现有研究在方法选择、数据集和实验协议方面缺乏统一标准，因此需要系统梳理研究现状并评估其可比性。

Method: 研究采用类似PRISMA 2020的系统性文献综述流程，检索ACM Digital Library、IEEE Xplore和Scopus数据库，经过筛选、去重和质量评估，从1358条记录中保留21项研究。随后对少样本学习方法、数据集、实验参数及报告性能进行分类和比较。

Result: 元学习和卷积神经网络是最常见的方法，分别出现在8项和10项研究中。多数研究采用每类不超过5个样本的实验设置，但具体任务配置差异较大。CIC-IDS2017和CSE-CIC-IDS2018是使用频率最高的数据集。部分研究缺失关键实验参数且未提供源代码，限制了结果复现和方法间的直接比较。

Conclusion: 网络入侵检测中的少样本学习研究正在发展，但目前缺乏统一、透明且可复现的评估规范。未来研究应明确报告数据划分、样本数量、任务构造、评价指标和实现代码，以提升研究结果的可比性与复现性。

Abstract: Anomaly-based network intrusion detection systems (NIDS) are an important first line of defense. However, training NIDS for new attack types is challenging, because labeled attack data are rarely available. Few-shot learning (FSL) addresses this problem by learning from few samples. However, the approaches and evaluation settings, that have been investigated so far, vary widely. This work systematically reviews FSL approaches for NIDS published from 2022 to 2026. We conduct a systematic literature review with PRISMA 2020-like reporting to search ACM Digital Library, IEEE Xplore, and Scopus. From a set of 1,358 initial records, we retain 21 studies after screening, deduplication, and quality filtering. We classify the applied FSL approaches, datasets, and experimental parameters and compare reported performance. Meta-learning and convolutional neural networks are the most common approaches, with 8 and 10 studies, respectively. Most studies evaluate five or fewer samples per class, although settings vary. CIC-IDS2017 and CSE-CIC-IDS2018 are the most frequently used datasets. Missing parameters and source code limit reproducibility and direct comparison between approaches.

</details>


### [114] [Deep-Fake CAPTCHA: Mitigating Next-Generation Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11404)
*Guy Frankovits,Lior Yasur,Fred M. Grabovski,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-CAPTCHA，一种用于语音和视频通话中实时深度伪造身份冒充的主动防御框架。系统通过要求通话者完成对人类简单但对实时深度伪造系统较难生成的挑战任务，并依据多项标准验证真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动深度伪造检测依赖媒体伪造痕迹，但实时生成技术不断进步，普通用户难以可靠区分真实内容与伪造内容。因此，需要一种不依赖固定伪造特征、能够在交互过程中主动验证身份的防御机制。

Method: DF-CAPTCHA采用挑战-响应机制，要求通话者完成简单任务，并从四个维度评估响应：真实性、身份一致性、任务完成情况和响应时间。研究同时覆盖音频和视频模态，通过用户研究以及真实实时深度伪造模型实验，将该方法与被动检测方法进行比较。

Result: 用户通常难以区分实时深度伪造媒体与真实媒体。实验结果表明，DF-CAPTCHA相较被动方法显著提升了检测性能，并在音频和视频两种模态中均达到较高准确率。摘要未报告具体数据、样本规模或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 主动式挑战验证能够有效增强实时深度伪造身份冒充的检测能力，具有较好的跨模态适用性和实际部署潜力。不过，其稳健性仍可能取决于任务设计、响应延迟、用户体验以及未来深度伪造系统对挑战任务的适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents DF-CAPTCHA, an active defense against real-time deepfake impersonation in voice and video calls. Instead of passively searching for artifacts, DF-CAPTCHA prompts the caller to perform simple challenge-response tasks that are easy for humans but difficult for current real-time deepfake systems to generate convincingly. The framework verifies the response using four criteria: realism, identity consistency, task completion, and response time. We evaluate the approach across both audio and video modalities using user studies and experiments with real-time deepfake models. Results show that people often struggle to distinguish real-time deepfakes from authentic media, while DF-CAPTCHA substantially improves detection performance over passive methods, reaching high accuracy in both modalities. These findings suggest that active challenge-based verification is a practical and robust defense against next-generation social engineering attacks based on real-time deepfakes.

</details>


### [115] ["They don't care about this": A Systematic Study of TEE Build Reproducibility in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11411)
*Annika Wilde,Marco Gutfleisch,Felix Reichmann,Anirban Chakraborty,Yuval Yarom,M. Angela Sasse,Ghassan Karame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了可信执行环境（TEE）部署的可复现构建状况，发现115个部署中91%不可复现，表明远程证明的可信验证基础普遍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程证明只有在验证者能够将TEE报告的测量值追溯到原始源代码时，才能真正证明代码的真实性与完整性，而这一能力依赖可复现构建。本文旨在评估当前TEE项目是否满足这一前提，并分析不可复现的原因。

Method: 研究者对115个Intel SGX、Intel TDX和AMD SEV等TEE部署进行了技术分析，重点检查源代码和参考构建是否公开。随后联系50个SGX项目维护者，并访谈来自产业界和学术界的12名开发者，以了解实际开发流程中对可复现性的重视程度及其障碍。

Result: 91%的TEE部署无法复现，80%同时缺少源代码和参考构建。访谈中仅有1名参与者表示可复现性是开发重点。不可复现原因既包括二进制中嵌入时间戳等可直接修复的技术问题，也包括多方协作项目中开发者无法控制完整构建环境等生态层面问题。

Conclusion: TEE生态目前普遍未将可复现构建作为远程证明的基础保障，导致测量值与源代码之间的可信关联不足。改善这一问题需要覆盖开发者、构建流程、协作方和项目治理的整体性方法，并建立比将TEE作为安全标识更强的工程与组织承诺。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have become a cornerstone of modern cloud computing, providing strong confidentiality and integrity guarantees for both code and data. A critical component of this trust model is remote attestation, which enables external entities to verify the authenticity and integrity of code executing within a TEE through cryptographic measurements. However, the effectiveness of remote attestation fundamentally depends on the verifier's ability to trace the reported measurement back to the original source code - a property that can only be guaranteed through reproducible builds.
  In this paper, we investigate the reproducibility of TEE builds through a technical analysis of 115 TEE deployments. Our analysis spans popular TEEs such as Intel SGX, Intel TDX, and AMD SEV, and reveals that a striking 91% of those deployments were not reproducible, with 80% failing to provide both source code and a reference build, the two essential prerequisites for reproducibility. To explore the root causes, we contacted the maintainers of 50 SGX projects and managed to recruit 12 developers from industry and academia for interviews. Only one of our participants reported that reproducibility is a priority during development, effectively confirming our technical findings. Beyond technical barriers (e.g., timestamps included in the binary) that can be readily addressed, we identify broader ecosystem-level challenges, such as the lack of control over the build environment in projects involving multiple stakeholders. We argue that achieving reproducibility in TEEs requires a holistic development approach that extends beyond individual developers and calls for stronger commitments - rather than treating TEEs as a "security badge".

</details>


### [116] [Heterogeneous Cross-Chain Transaction Tracing for Solana Bridges via Candidate-Set Selective Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11413)
*Wenjie Dou,Zheng Che,Meng Shen,Hanbiao Du,Qing Li,Yan Qiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Solana is a rapidly growing high-throughput blockchain platform that has attracted substantial liquidity and user activity. However, this expansion has also drawn the attention of illicit actors, who frequently leverage cross-chain bridges to route illicit funds onto Solana to obfuscate transaction lineage. Unlike EVM-compatible platforms, Solana features distinct execution dynamics and lacks standard event logs, creating severe semantic gaps that prevent existing tracing methods from reliably correlating cross-ledger transactions. In this paper, we formalize four types of Solana-bound cross-chain transaction modes and propose a candidate-set selective decision-based tracing method called SolTracer. SolTracer maps disparate execution semantics into a unified event space and employs candidate-set selective decision-making to reliably associate target transactions while abstaining when valid targets are absent. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SolTracer outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across three representative scenarios: closed-world association, open-world association, and cross-source-chain generalization. In particular, under the challenging open-world setting with a 50% TA ratio, SolTracer improves the F1 score by 20.16% over the strongest SOTA baseline. Utilizing SolTracer, we conduct an empirical analysis on real-world cross-chain transfers to investigate ecosystem dynamics. Our analysis explores the stark count-value divergence across bridge mechanisms, the prevalence of cross-asset shifts, and the decoupling between on-chain settlement and explorer visibility.

</details>


### [117] [Chypothermia: Clock Freezing for Static Side-channel Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11442)
*Fatemeh Khojasteh Dana,Mehmet Ali Cetin,Xinrui Wang,Andrew Butler,Yuval Yarom,Shahin Tajik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出低温侧信道攻击 Chypothermia，通过削弱芯片上的混合信号组件，禁用时钟、时钟传感器和电压传感器，从而在不清除秘密数据的情况下实现停钟攻击；结合 Chypnosis 后，可在规避温度检测的条件下攻击配备多类防护的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态侧信道攻击利用停钟状态提取敏感信息，而现有防御通常依赖时钟异常检测并触发敏感数据清除。研究旨在评估低温环境对这些检测与响应机制的影响，并验证其对实际可信硬件平台的威胁。

Method: 研究分析低温对片上混合信号电路的影响，构造 Chypothermia 以禁用时钟传感器、时钟生成电路和电压传感器，同时保持秘密数据。随后将其与 Chypnosis 组合，以降低温度异常的可检测性；在多个 FPGA/SoC 平台上测试软 IP 与硬 IP 传感器，并针对 OpenTitan 根信任的告警处理器进行实证评估。最后设计并实现 FPGA 兼容的自加热传感器作为防御方案。

Result: Chypothermia 成功禁用了多种 FPGA/SoC 平台上的软 IP 和硬 IP 传感器，并在 OpenTitan 根信任中规避检测、阻止密钥清零。与 Chypnosis 联合后，即使在相对温和的低温工作范围内，也能实现停钟且逃避温度异常检测。实验还表明，所提出的自加热传感器能够抵抗 Chypothermia。

Conclusion: 低温环境会显著削弱依赖片上混合信号电路的时钟、电压和异常检测机制，使静态侧信道防御面临实际风险。单纯依赖外部温度检测不足以保证安全；具备主动热响应能力的自加热传感器可作为更稳健的缓解措施。

Abstract: Static side-channel attacks, which exploit halted-clock conditions to extract sensitive information, pose an increasing threat to chip security. To counter these attacks, various defenses have been proposed that monitor for abnormal clock behavior and trigger the clearing of sensitive data when clock anomalies are detected. In this work, we demonstrate that exposing a chip to cryogenic temperatures interferes with on-chip mixed-signal components, responsible for signal sensing and generation. Based on this observation, we develop Chypothermia, an attack that, without any electrical tampering with the system, disables the target clock sensor, the clock generation circuit, and the voltage sensors, while preserving the secret data.
  While effective at halting the clock, cooling is a slow process and, on its own, is often insufficient against systems equipped with temperature sensors designed to detect thermal anomalies. To bypass these protections, we combine Chypothermia with Chypnosis (Mitard et al., IEEE SP 2026) and show that, even within a moderately low-temperature operating range, this combination can halt the clock while evading detection. We implement Chypothermia on multiple FPGA/SoC platforms and demonstrate successful disabling of both soft-IP and hard-IP sensor implementations. Finally, we apply Chypothermia to the alert handler of the OpenTitan root of trust, which incorporates a state-of-the-art clock sensor, and show that the attack evades detection and prevents key zeroization. Finally, we introduce and implement an FPGA-compatible self-heating sensor as a countermeasure and demonstrate its robustness against Chypothermia.

</details>


### [118] [On Identifying Sound Conditions for Frontrunning Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11535)
*Sebastian Holler,Anna Piscitelli,Jannik Albrecht,Stephan Dübler,Ghassan Karame,Clara Schneidewind*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化定义智能合约中的抢跑漏洞，指出其安全性取决于诚实用户的交互方式，而非仅由合约本身决定；同时提出可综合安全交互条件的可靠算法，并在真实以太坊合约中发现了此前未识别的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易排序具有异步性，矿工可通过重排、延迟或插入交易实施抢跑攻击。现有研究缺乏严格的漏洞定义，动态检测方法覆盖不足：在287份合约审计报告的393个漏洞中，55%不符合现有检测标准。因此，需要建立形式化定义和更全面的分析方法。

Method: 研究首先将抢跑抵抗性建模为合约与诚实用户交互方式共同决定的性质，而非合约的固有属性。在此基础上，提出智能合约抢跑漏洞的形式化定义，并设计用于综合安全交互条件的可靠算法，进一步实现原型工具并将其应用于经过审计的真实合约。

Result: 对287份审计报告的分析表明，现有最先进检测标准无法覆盖55%的已报告漏洞。原型工具在真实以太坊合约上的实验中发现了两个此前未被识别的漏洞，验证了形式化定义和算法的实际检测能力。

Conclusion: 该研究弥补了智能合约抢跑漏洞在定义和检测方面的基础缺口，表明评估抢跑安全性必须同时考虑合约语义与用户交互条件。所提出的形式化框架和安全条件综合算法为系统化分析及构建更安全的区块链应用提供了基础。

Abstract: Blockchains enable decentralized applications through smart contracts---interactive programs executed through consensus. However, the inherently asynchronous nature of blockchain transaction ordering introduces a class of vulnerabilities known as frontrunning attacks, which have caused millions of dollars in losses in major blockchains, such as Ethereum. Frontrunning attacks arise because users interact with smart contracts through transactions, which are added to the blockchain by designated nodes called miners. Miners can exploit their ability to reorder, delay, or insert transactions to gain an advantage over honest users, effectively frontrunning them.
  Yet, to date, the field lacks a rigorous definition of what it even means for a contract to resist such attacks. Worse, we show that existing dynamic detection approaches are fundamentally inadequate: in a large-scale study comprising 287 smart contract audits, 55% of the 393 reported vulnerabilities identified by leading smart contract auditors fall outside the scope of state-of-the-art detection criteria. To address this gap, we propose the first formal definition of frontrunning vulnerability for smart contracts. Our definition captures a key insight: resistance to frontrunning is not an intrinsic property of a contract alone, but depends critically on how honest users interact with it. Grounded in this observation, we develop a sound algorithm for synthesizing secure interaction conditions, alongside a prototype implementation that we apply to audited real-world contracts---revealing previously undiscovered vulnerabilities in two Ethereum contracts.

</details>


### [119] [Accountability in Certificate Transparency and Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11552)
*Timo Treitz,Robert Künnemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在Dolev–Yao模型下系统分析证书透明度（CT）的信任假设与问责性，发现基础CT依赖诚实日志，但在该假设下能够实现问责；SCT审计可消除对诚实日志的依赖，而Gossiping扩展无法做到这一点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT分析通常采用过度简化的系统模型，并使用针对证书颁发机构设计的定义，难以准确刻画CT以复杂基础设施换取信任削减的实际安全目标。本文将问责性确立为CT的核心目标，以明确不同参与角色之间的信任关系及其安全保证。

Method: 作者在Dolev–Yao符号模型中，从传统PKI出发，逐步引入CT机制，并依次分析基础CT、SCT审计扩展和Gossiping扩展。分析覆盖CA、日志服务器、监控方及终端客户端之间的检查、证据和信任依赖。

Result: 基础CT仍要求至少存在诚实日志，但在此条件下能够对不当证书签发或日志行为提供问责。SCT审计扩展能够进一步消除对诚实日志的信任假设；Gossiping扩展则不能消除该假设。

Conclusion: CT并非无条件地消除对CA及相关基础设施的信任，而是将安全性转化为可验证的问责性。不同扩展的效果存在本质差异：SCT审计在信任削减方面有效，Gossiping主要增强检测或一致性验证能力，却不足以独立实现无诚实日志假设的问责保证。

Abstract: Certificate Transparency (CT) aims to reduce the trust required in Certificate Authorities (CAs) within the TLS certificate ecosystem. It is supported by all major browsers. The protocol obliges all CAs to record the certificates they issue in a public log, which itself is monitored for compliance and consistency by third parties. Given this complex set of checks between the four roles-CA, loggers, monitor but also the end user's client-it is very hard to provide a precise account of how CT eliminates trust assumptions in exchange for complex infrastructure. Analyses both in the Dolev-Yao paradigm and the computational paradigm only regard a very simplified model and feature definitions adapted specifically to CAs, essentially capturing design features rather than the target property. The present paper posits accountability as the main goal of CT and presents a thorough analysis in the Dolev-Yao model. We start with the vanilla PKI and, step by step, move to CT, finally analyzing proposed extensions for SCT Auditing and Gossiping. We show that plain CT relies on an honest log, but provides accountability under this assumption. Furthermore, we show that the SCT Auditing extension can eliminate this assumption, while the Gossiping extension cannot.

</details>


### [120] [From Intent to Execution Grant: An Execution-Boundary Conformance Profile for High-Risk AI Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11596)
*Mengting Wu,Lin Wang,Yong Zhang,Jiang Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 EBL-Core，一种面向 AI 生成候选行动的执行边界一致性规范，用于在明确条件下判断单一、完整物化的候选行动是否可获得受行动约束的执行权限。其核心是执行释放合约（ERC），并通过策略、证据、上下文、时间和可验证决策推导连接候选行动与后续执行授权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有授权引擎、策略语言、运行时监控、溯源机制和代理护栏虽能提供基础能力，但通常未定义从特定候选行动到实际执行权限之间的统一语义契约。EBL-Core旨在填补这一执行边界缺口，降低策略弱化、证据处理不一致、授权生命周期竞态及行动绑定不明确等风险。

Method: 规范定义了结构化意图对象、根策略与操作策略、证据义务、类型化证据、上下文、时间和可验证决策推导，并将其绑定到执行释放合约（ERC）。该合约支持行动绑定、策略不可弱化、证据处理、确定性裁决、推导验证及授权授予生命周期管理。参考实现包含模式定义、裁决器、独立验证器、语义重放机制和线性化内存授权存储；ERC本身不携带执行权限，验证通过的ALLOW ERC还需在兑换时接受独立的执行授予验证。

Result: 保留运行中，34个静态测试向量和15个生命周期检查均得到预期结果。在100次并发测试中，每次32个并发兑换请求均恰好产生一次成功兑换，并保护了对应的测试效果；在100次撤销与兑换竞态测试中，所有结果均进入有效终态。实验支持该规范子集具备可执行性，但未证明人工意图正确性、证据真实性、完整调解能力、生产就绪性、机械化正确性或部署级安全性。

Conclusion: EBL-Core为AI候选行动获得执行权限建立了较明确、可验证且具有生命周期语义的执行边界契约。参考实现和有界测试表明其在指定场景下能够实现确定性裁决及并发安全的授权兑换，但证据范围有限，距离验证真实意图、保证外部证据可信及满足生产环境安全要求仍有明显差距。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly propose actions with external consequences, including financial transfers, infrastructure changes, software deployments, disclosures, and physical actuation. Authorization engines, policy languages, runtime monitors, provenance mechanisms, and agent guardrails provide important foundations, but do not necessarily define a common semantic contract for the final transition from a particular candidate action to execution authority.
  We specify EBL-Core, an execution-boundary conformance profile for deciding whether one canonical, fully materialized AI-generated candidate may receive action-scoped execution authority under explicit conditions. It binds a structured intent object, Root and Operational Policies, evidence obligations, typed evidence, context, time, and a verifiable Decision Derivation through an Execution Release Contract (ERC). An ERC is not an authority-bearing token; a verified ALLOW ERC may support a separate Execution Grant governed by Redemption-time validation.
  EBL-Core specifies action binding, policy non-weakening, evidence handling, deterministic adjudication, derivation verification, and grant lifecycle behavior. An accompanying reference artifact provides schemas, adjudication, separate verification and Semantic Replay, and a linearizable in-memory grant store. In the retained run, 34 static vectors and 15 lifecycle checks matched expected outcomes. Across 100 trials, 32 concurrent Redemption attempts yielded exactly one successful Redemption and protected test effect per trial; 100 Revoke-Redeem races ended in valid terminal outcomes. These bounded results demonstrate executability of the specified subset, not human-intent correctness, evidence truth, complete mediation, production readiness, mechanized correctness, or deployment-level security.

</details>


### [121] [PHAT: PHotonic Accelerator for TFHE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11613)
*Guowei Yang,Farbin Fayza,Beren Aydoğan,Carlos A. Ríos Ocampo,Ayse K. Coskun,Ajay Joshi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出PHAT，一种利用光寻址相变存储器（OPCM）的光电混合TFHE加速器，通过存内计算加速自举过程中的FFT，在四个真实TFHE工作负载上较先进ASIC实现2.14至5.10倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFHE支持任意同态操作，但自举阶段所需FFT带来显著计算、通信和存储访问开销。传统电子加速器受技术缩放瓶颈和存储墙限制，难以提供足够吞吐率，因此需要利用高带宽、近数据计算的光电存内计算架构。

Method: PHAT采用OPCM构建FFT处理单元，并设计面向OPCM特性的twiddle-stationary数据流，以减少数据移动和提高计算复用率。同时，通过专用调度机制提升FFT单元利用率。针对OPCM直接映射FFT时存在的高精度模拟计算、单元编程延迟高及能耗高等问题，论文采用电光协同架构进行优化。

Result: 在四个真实TFHE工作负载上，PHAT相较当前先进ASIC加速器实现2.14至5.10倍性能提升。结果表明，OPCM存内计算能够有效提高TFHE中FFT操作的计算与通信吞吐率。摘要未报告面积、能耗、精度误差或编程开销等具体定量结果。

Conclusion: PHAT证明了OPCM与光电协同设计用于TFHE加速的可行性。通过FFT专用硬件、匹配的数据流和调度策略，该方案显著缓解了TFHE的性能瓶颈，为云端隐私计算的实用化提供了潜在路径，但其实际部署仍需进一步评估模拟精度、器件编程成本、可靠性及系统级能效。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables secure computation on encrypted data, making it a promising solution for privacy-preserving applications in the cloud. Among various FHE schemes, FHE over the Torus (TFHE) stands out due to its support for arbitrary operations. However, its high computation and communication overhead, particularly in the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) operations required during bootstrapping, limits its practicality for real-world applications. Conventional electronic accelerators struggle to achieve sufficient throughput due to the limitations of technology scaling and the memory-wall problem.
  To address these challenges, we propose PHAT, a PHotonic Accelerator for TFHE leveraging Optically-addressed Phase-Change Memory (OPCM). OPCM-based processing-in-memory systems offer high computation and communication throughput, making them well-suited for accelerating FFT operations in TFHE. However, directly mapping FFT to OPCM presents challenges such as high-precision analog computation and the high latency and energy cost of programming OPCM cells. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel electro-photonic accelerator architecture optimized for TFHE, featuring OPCM-based FFT units, a twiddle-stationary dataflow tailored for OPCM, and a scheduling mechanism to maximize the utilization of the FFT units. PHAT delivers $2.14\times$--$5.10\times$ speedup across four real-world TFHE workloads against the state-of-the-art ASIC accelerator. Our approach significantly enhances the performance of TFHE applications, paving the way for practical and efficient homomorphic encryption in cloud computing.

</details>


### [122] [Signing the Transaction but Not the Decision: Whisper Attacks and a Binding Defense for AP2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11757)
*Yedidel Louck,Amit Dvir,Ariel Stulman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了代理支付协议AP2的核心缺陷：协议能够验证购买签名，却无法约束购物代理形成购物车时的决策过程。攻击者可利用普通商品描述文本诱导代理偏离用户意图，并提出A-VIP协议层防御机制进行约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理支付协议主要验证签名和交易结果的密码学有效性，却未验证购物车是否源自用户真实意图。商品描述中的普通事实性文本因此可能成为操纵代理决策的攻击面，导致凭证误取、展示内容与实际购买内容不一致，或未经授权的额外支出。

Method: 研究构造并实证评估三类攻击：诱导代理获取其他用户的支付凭证；生成与用户所见商品不一致但密码学有效的购物车；通过关于库存或产品 lineage 的单一事实性陈述，将代理从低价展示商品引导至高价商品。实验覆盖AP2默认使用的Gemini Flash-Lite模型、17个Google模型、3种代理框架、2个跨厂商锚点以及Google消费级助手。研究进一步提出A-VIP，将签名意图视为能力授权，并把凭证查询绑定至发起请求的会话、把购物车条目绑定至代理所见商品列表，同时检测未经授权的支出；此外发布代码、机器检查不变量和包含1544个场景的AP2-WhisperBench。

Result: 三类攻击成功率分别为90%、56%和73.3%，且在不同模型、代理框架和产品环境中均观察到类似漏洞。A-VIP通过结构绑定以零误报阻断前两类攻击；第三类攻击不存在可检测的结构痕迹，因此由系统识别未经授权的支出并要求用户确认。

Conclusion: 代理支付的密码学有效性不足以保证交易符合用户意图，协议必须同时约束凭证访问、商品来源和支出授权。A-VIP提供了较有针对性的协议层防御，但对于无法从交易结构中识别的语义操纵，需要保留用户确认机制。发布的实现与基准测试有助于系统评估此类代理购物安全问题。

Abstract: Software agents are beginning to shop and pay on a person's behalf. Agent payment protocols such as AP2 produce cryptographically valid signatures for completed purchases, yet do not constrain the decisions that lead to them. Consequently, ordinary product-description text can steer a shopping agent into forming a cart that passes every protocol check but no longer matches the user's request. In this paper, we show that this vulnerability enables three related attacks. In the first attack, the agent is steered into fetching another user's payment credentials. In the second, it assembles a cryptographically valid cart whose contents do not match what the user was shown. In the third, a single factual claim about stock or product lineage moves the agent from the cheaper displayed item to a more expensive one, while the resulting cart remains fully consistent with the listing. In experiments using the Gemini Flash-Lite models that AP2's sample agents specify by default, the three attacks succeeded at rates of 90%, 56%, and 73.3%, respectively. The same vulnerability appears across seventeen Google models, three unrelated agent frameworks, two cross-vendor anchors, and Google's own consumer assistant. To address this attack vector, we introduce A-VIP (AP2 Verified-Intent Protection), a protocol-layer defense that treats the signed intent as a capability grant rather than judging the merchant's description. The defense binds every credential lookup to the session that requested it and every cart line to the listing seen, while flagging unauthorized spending. The first two attacks leave structural traces that these bindings block with zero false positives. The third attack leaves no trace, so A-VIP surfaces unauthorized spending for user confirmation. Finally, we release the A-VIP code, machine-checked invariants, and AP2-WhisperBench, a suite of 1,544 evaluation scenarios.

</details>


### [123] [Differentially Private EEG Feature Anonymization: A Privacy-Utility Case Study in Clinical Neurophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11777)
*Noman Sadiq,Mohsen Toorani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在临床脑电图（EEG）特征表示上应用主体级差分隐私，通过高斯和拉普拉斯扰动降低患者信息泄露风险，并评估隐私保护与数据效用之间的权衡。结果表明，差分隐私可嵌入EEG处理流程，但噪声机制、隐私参数和敏感度校准会显著影响后续分析与机器学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG及其派生特征具有较高研究和人工智能应用价值，但可能包含可识别患者的敏感信息。传统去标识化难以防范高维生物医学信号中的重识别、关联和推断风险，而过强的隐私扰动又可能破坏临床相关信号特征，因此需要系统评估EEG特征匿名化中的隐私—效用平衡。

Method: 研究采用主体级差分隐私保护患者级EEG特征表示，比较高斯扰动和拉普拉斯扰动。框架覆盖客户端匿名化、集中式服务器端匿名化和去中心化本地训练三种部署场景。流程包括EEG预处理、特征提取和特征级噪声注入；拉普拉斯实验评估实际实施的噪声尺度，同时单独推导满足完整向量校准要求的噪声尺度。效用评估结合统计指标和下游机器学习任务表现。

Result: 差分隐私扰动能够集成到临床EEG处理流程中，但效用受扰动机制、隐私参数和敏感度校准的显著影响。不同设置下，扰动可能改变特征分布并降低下游机器学习性能。小样本且类别不平衡的临床EEG数据对噪声更加敏感，导致隐私保护与任务效用之间的冲突更突出。摘要未报告具体隐私预算、定量效用指标或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 主体级差分隐私为临床EEG特征共享和再利用提供了可行的隐私保护路径，但尚不能保证在强隐私约束下维持稳定的临床或机器学习效用。实际部署需要根据应用场景选择扰动机制，严格进行向量级敏感度校准，并针对小样本和类别不平衡问题优化隐私预算与数据处理流程。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) data are valuable for healthcare research and for developing artificial intelligence (AI)-based clinical decision-support systems, but EEG recordings and derived features may contain sensitive patient-specific information. This creates privacy risks when data are reused, analyzed, or shared across clinical and research environments. Conventional anonymization methods are often insufficient for high-dimensional biomedical signals, since removing direct identifiers does not necessarily prevent re-identification, linkage, or inference risks. At the same time, strong privacy protection may distort clinically relevant signal characteristics and reduce data utility. This paper studies subject-level differential privacy for protecting clinical EEG-derived feature representations using Gaussian and Laplace perturbations. The proposed framework considers three deployment scenarios: client-side anonymization, centralized server-side anonymization, and decentralized local training. Following EEG preprocessing and feature extraction, Gaussian and Laplace perturbations are applied to the resulting patient-level EEG feature representations. The Laplace experiments evaluate the implemented noise scales, while the scales required for formal full-vector calibration are derived separately. The effects of both perturbations are assessed using statistical utility measures and a downstream machine-learning-based utility check. The results show that differentially private perturbation can be integrated into EEG processing workflows, but the selected mechanism, privacy parameters, and sensitivity calibration strongly influence data utility. The study highlights the practical privacy-utility trade-off in DP-based EEG feature anonymization and the challenges of preserving downstream utility in small and imbalanced clinical EEG datasets.

</details>


### [124] [SpecGuard: Inference-Time Backdoor Detection For Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11799)
*Rui Wen,Ahmed Salem,Andrew Paverd,Mark Russinovich,Zheng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecGuard，一种利用推测解码过程中已有的令牌接受率信号，在不增加模型计算成本的情况下检测大语言模型运行时后门触发的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方微调、共享或下载的模型可能携带隐藏后门。现有运行时检测器要么依赖特定触发器形式、难以识别隐蔽攻击，要么需要输入扰动或额外生成过程，增加延迟和计算开销。因此，需要一种适用于延迟敏感服务、且无需额外模型推理的检测机制。

Method: SpecGuard复用推测解码流程：由较小的草稿模型生成候选令牌，再由目标模型验证。当后门触发时，目标模型会转向攻击者控制的行为，而干净的草稿模型通常不会同步产生该转变，导致草稿令牌接受率发生变化。论文形式化分析了该信号出现的条件，并论证攻击者若试图抑制该信号，也必须削弱后门效果。

Result: 在多种后门类型和模型家族上的实验表明，SpecGuard能够可靠检测已触发的后门行为，包括输入级过滤器难以识别的隐蔽攻击，同时不引入额外生成过程或模型计算成本。

Conclusion: 推测解码不仅能够加速推理，还可作为持续运行的后门检测信号。SpecGuard在保持服务延迟和计算开销不变的前提下，为大语言模型部署提供了一种实用的运行时安全监测方案。

Abstract: Large language models are often fine-tuned, shared, or downloaded from third parties, so a deployed model may carry a hidden backdoor that behaves normally on benign inputs but switches to attacker-controlled behavior when a secret trigger appears. While backdoors can be audited before deployment, runtime monitoring remains important for models that are frequently updated. The challenge is that LLM serving is latency-sensitive: existing inference-time detectors either rely on assumptions about the trigger form, which can fail on stealthy attacks, or require extra model computation, such as input perturbations or an additional generation pass.
  We introduce SpecGuard, an inference-time backdoor detector that repurposes speculative decoding at zero added model-computation cost. Speculative decoding speeds up inference by using a small draft model to propose tokens and a target model to verify them. We observe that this verification process already exposes a useful signal: when a backdoor is triggered, the target model shifts toward the attacker's behavior, while a clean draft model does not predict this shift, causing the draft-token acceptance rate to change.
  We formalize when this signal appears and show that an attacker who suppresses it must also weaken the backdoor. Across diverse backdoor types and model families, SpecGuard reliably detects triggered behavior, including stealthy cases where input-level filters are blind, while avoiding the extra generation cost of existing runtime detectors. Speculative decoding therefore doubles as a free, always-on signal for detecting backdoored LLM behavior.

</details>


### [125] [Don't Trust the Super-App: A Case Study of Russia's Max](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11814)
*Richa Priyanka,Aaron Ortwein,Joel Reardon,Michael Specter,Piyush Kumar Sharma,Roya Ensafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该摘要指出，超级应用作为小程序的宿主，被既有研究默认视为可信中介；论文则论证这一信任前提缺乏充分依据，并认为宿主层的架构特权可被滥用，从而在不易被小程序或用户察觉的情况下破坏安全与隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小程序依赖超级应用提供运行时、存储、网络与认证等基础能力，因此其安全边界并不完全由小程序自身控制。作者旨在纠正研究与生态中“宿主天然可信”的隐含假设，揭示由宿主架构地位带来的系统性风险。

Method: 论文以一个具体超级应用为案例，分析其对小程序界面、本地存储、JavaScript运行时、网络通信和认证上下文的控制能力；再将这些能力归因于超级应用通用的架构权限，而非单一产品实现缺陷，并据此提出移动操作系统与应用商店层面的干预需求。

Result: 作者声称恶意超级应用能够截取小程序界面、读取或修改本地存储、向运行时注入任意JavaScript、调解网络流量并控制认证上下文。这些能力可导致隐蔽的身份冒用及数据安全风险，且相关行为可能不留下可供用户或小程序识别的痕迹。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：超级应用宿主不应再被视为默认可信的安全边界。由于问题源于宿主在设计上拥有的广泛特权，单靠小程序开发者难以缓解；应在移动操作系统和应用分发平台层面引入更强的隔离、权限约束、可审计性与透明度机制。

Abstract: Super-apps, an emerging mobile architecture, host third-party mini-apps inside a single app, allowing users to access diverse services. A decade of security research on the super-app ecosystem has all assumed super-apps to be a trusted intermediary. We argue this implicit trust is difficult to justify: China's WeChat is already shown to passively track its user's activity across mini-apps at extraordinary scale; Russia's MAX's parent company is reported to be deeply entangled with the state prosecution of online speech; and Iran's Bale was reported to be functioning in the world's longest internet shutdown due to its state-backed support.
  In this paper, we show how malicious super-apps have undeniable capabilities to silently undermine the security and privacy of mini-apps and users without leaving any trace. Using MAX as an example, we show how it can capture mini-app UI, read and write mini-app local storage, inject arbitrary JavaScript into a mini-app's runtime, mediate mini-app network traffic, and control authentication context in ways that can enable silent user impersonation. Sadly, these capabilities manifest themselves in any super-app because of the architectural privileges granted to them by design. We argue that mobile OS and app store interventions are urgently needed to close this architectural blind spot before it is further exploited.

</details>


### [126] [Atlas: Efficient Verifiable Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11841)
*Nikolay Avramov,Hidde Lycklama,Alexander Viand,Anwar Hithnawi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Atlas提出一种面向HNSW语义搜索的零知识证明系统，使服务提供方能够证明查询结果确实依据约定算法和已承诺索引生成，同时不泄露索引内容。系统在SIFT1M上可将单次查询证明时间控制在1秒内，并在1亿向量规模下达到2.0秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义搜索通常由服务提供方控制索引和查询执行，客户端无法确认搜索是否使用了正确算法、完整索引或未经截断的执行过程。已有可验证检索方案主要采用规则化、基于聚类的索引，便于编码但通常牺牲图搜索的召回率。因此，需要一种既支持高召回HNSW搜索、又能高效生成零知识证明的方案。

Method: Atlas围绕HNSW搜索构建新的零知识证明机制。第一，通过预处理将与数据库相关的计算成本转移到离线阶段，使在线证明成本主要随实际搜索遍历规模增长，而非随数据库规模增长。第二，将HNSW重构为固定大小状态的程序，并证明该重构与原始HNSW产生相同结果。第三，引入带时间步标签的批处理，将整个搜索遍历中各步骤的证明合并为单个证明。系统同时对索引进行承诺，并通过零知识机制隐藏索引内容。

Result: 在SIFT1M基准上，Atlas可在1秒内完成查询证明；在1亿向量规模下，证明时间为2.0秒。其召回率保持与明文HNSW一致，并且除查询结果外不泄露索引信息。在完整的检索增强生成流程中，Atlas能够保持端到端答案质量，并以更低证明成本获得高于已有可验证检索系统的答案质量。

Conclusion: Atlas首次展示了具备规模化可验证性的图结构语义搜索。其核心贡献是将HNSW的数据依赖遍历转化为适合零知识证明的固定状态流程，并通过离线预处理和批处理降低证明开销。结果表明，可验证检索不必以牺牲HNSW的召回率或RAG质量为代价，但摘要未详细说明证明系统的具体密码学构造、硬件配置、证明大小及验证时间，实际部署成本仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Semantic search is a core primitive of modern applications, powering recommender systems, web search, and retrieval-augmented generation for language models. The provider controls the index and query execution, leaving clients to trust that results come from the right algorithm over the intended index. A provider may truncate search to cut cost, bias results, or otherwise deviate from the specified execution undetected. Verifiability can remove this trust assumption by proving that results follow the agreed algorithm over a committed index. Realizing this efficiently is hard, as retrieval at scale relies on HNSW, a graph-based algorithm whose data-dependent traversal maps poorly onto the fixed constraint systems of zero-knowledge proofs. Prior verifiable systems therefore target regular, cluster-based indices that are easier to encode, sacrificing the recall of graph-based search. We present Atlas, a system that lets a provider prove a query was answered correctly against its committed index without revealing the index.
